                                    local hrp = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    if hrp then
                                        pcall(function()
                                            toy:PivotTo(hrp.CFrame)
                                        end)
                                        visited[p.UserId] = true
                                        task.wait(0.2)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            local allVisited = true
                            for _, p in ipairs(list) do
                                if not visited[p.UserId] then
                                    allVisited = false
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                            if allVisited and #list > 0 then
                                visited = {}
                            end
                        end
                        task.wait()
                    end
                end))
            end
            local function onToyAdded(child)
                if child.Name == "OvenDarkGray" and active then
                    currentToy = child
                    visited = {}
                    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                        task.wait(0.5)
                        if not active or currentToy ~= child then
                            return
                        end
                        setOwner(child)
                        toyActive = true
                        loop(child)
                    end))
                end
            end
            local toysFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                or workspace:WaitForChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys", 5)
            if not toysFolder then
                error("SpawnedInToys 폴더를 찾을 수 없습니다.")
            end
            env.Loopovenall = {Stop = Stop}
            table.insert(connections, getgenv().__DST(toysFolder.ChildAdded:Connect(onToyAdded)))
            table.insert(connections, getgenv().__DST(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if active and (not currentToy or not currentToy.Parent) then
                    currentToy = nil
                    visited = {}
                    spawnToy()
                end
            end)))
            spawnToy()
        end)
        if ovenOk then
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ Oven All ]", Text = "시작했습니다. .unovenall로 중지", Duration = 3})
        else
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ Oven All 오류 ]", Text = tostring(ovenErr), Duration = 4})
        end
        return
    elseif lowerMsg == ".unovenall" then
        local genv = getgenv()
        if genv.Loopovenall then
            genv.Loopovenall.Stop()
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ Oven All 종료 ]", Text = "중지했습니다.", Duration = 3})
        else
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ Oven All ]", Text = "실행중이 아닙니다.", Duration = 3})
        end
        return
    elseif lowerMsg == ".shutdown" then
        if game.PlaceId ~= 6961824067 then
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ Everyone ]", Text = "이 게임에서는 사용할 수 없습니다.", Duration = 3})
            return
        end
        getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
            local everyoneOk, everyoneErr = pcall(function()
                (loadstring(game:HttpGet('https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dosi1100-cmyk/scripts/refs/heads/main/shutdown')) :: () -> any)()
            end)
            if not everyoneOk then
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ Everyone 오류 ]", Text = tostring(everyoneErr), Duration = 4})
            end
        end))
        return
    elseif lowerMsg == ".cleanall" then
        getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
            if not game:IsLoaded() then
                game.Loaded:Wait()
            end

            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
            local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")

            local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

            local function notify(title, text, duration)
                pcall(function()
                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                        Title = title or "DShub",
                        Text = text or "Notification",
                        Duration = duration or 5,
                    })
                end)
            end

            if game.PlaceId ~= 6961824067 then
                notify("DShub", "This game is not supported; couldn't start the script", 3)
                return
            end

            local env = getgenv and getgenv() or _G
            if env.DestroyAllToysRunning then
                return
            end
            env.DestroyAllToysRunning = true

            local grabEvents = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GrabEvents", 5)
            local setNetworkOwner = grabEvents and grabEvents:WaitForChild("SetNetworkOwner", 5)
            local destroyGrabLine = grabEvents and grabEvents:WaitForChild("DestroyGrabLine", 5)

            if not (setNetworkOwner and destroyGrabLine) then
                env.DestroyAllToysRunning = false
                notify("DShub", "Grab remotes not found", 3)
                return
            end

            local destroyThread = nil
            local owning = false
            local voidHeight = 0 / 0
            local originalHeight = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
            local partOwnerCache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
            local grabbableParts = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

            local function getChar()
                local character = localPlayer.Character
                if not character then
                    return
                end
                local root = character:FindFirstChild("realHrp") or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                return character, root, humanoid
            end

            local function getMagnitude(a, b)
                if not a or not b then
                    return
                end
                return (a.Position - b.Position).Magnitude
            end

            local function isPartOwner(part)
                if not (part and part:IsA("BasePart")) then
                    return
                end
                local cached = partOwnerCache[part]
                if cached and cached.Parent then
                    return cached.Value == localPlayer.Name
                end
                local parent = part.Parent
                local head = part.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" and parent and parent:FindFirstChild("Head")
                head = head and head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                if head and head.Value == localPlayer.Name then
                    partOwnerCache[part] = head
                    return true
                end
                local partOwner = part:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                if partOwner and partOwner.Value == localPlayer.Name then
                    partOwnerCache[part] = partOwner
                    return true
                end
                parent = parent and parent:FindFirstChild("PartOwner", true)
                if parent then
                    partOwnerCache[part] = parent
                    return parent.Value == localPlayer.Name
                end
                return false
            end

            local function grab(part, cf)
                local _, root, humanoid = getChar()
                if not root or not humanoid or humanoid.Health == 0 or not part or not part.Parent or not part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    return
                end
                local base = (cf or root.CFrame) * CFrame.new(0, 1.593, 0)
                local aim = CFrame.new(base.Position, part.CFrame.Position)
                setNetworkOwner:FireServer(part, aim)
            end

            local function takeOwnership(part)
                local character, root, humanoid = getChar()
                local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                local ragdolled = humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                if not (head and ragdolled and root and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0) then
                    return false
                end

                if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") or not part.Parent or getMagnitude(part, (workspace :: any).SpawnLocation) > 10000000 then
                    return false
                end

                if isPartOwner(part) then
                    return true
                end

                if owning then
                    return false
                end

                local far = false
                local headCFrame = CFrame.new()
                local rootCFrame = CFrame.new()
                local targetCFrame = CFrame.new()
                local connection = nil
                local jitterSign = 1
                local jitterStep = 0.02

                if (root.Position - part.Position).Magnitude >= 31 then
                    far = true
                end

                owning = true

                local function startMask()
                    rootCFrame = root.CFrame
                    headCFrame = head.CFrame
                    targetCFrame = root.CFrame

                    RunService:BindToRenderStep("DestroyAllToysClient", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
                        root.CFrame = rootCFrame
                        head.CFrame = headCFrame
                    end)

                    connection = getgenv().__DST(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                        rootCFrame = root.CFrame
                        headCFrame = head.CFrame
                        jitterSign = -jitterSign
                        local jitterCFrame = targetCFrame * CFrame.new(jitterStep * jitterSign, 0, jitterStep * jitterSign)
                        root.CFrame = jitterCFrame
                        head.CFrame = jitterCFrame
                    end))
                end

                local function stopMask()
                    RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("DestroyAllToysClient")

                    if connection then
                        connection:Disconnect()
                        connection = nil
                    end

                    head.CFrame = headCFrame
                    root.CFrame = rootCFrame
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end

                local ok, result = pcall(function()
                    if far then
                        startMask()
                    end

                    local startTime = tick()

                    while part.Parent and not isPartOwner(part) and humanoid.Health ~= 0 and root.Parent and tick() - startTime < 0.65 do
                        if far then
                            targetCFrame = part.CFrame - Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                        end

                        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()

                        local passCFrame = nil
                        if far then
                            passCFrame = targetCFrame
                        end

                        grab(part, passCFrame)
                    end
                end)

                if far then
                    pcall(stopMask)
                end

                owning = false

                if not ok then
                    return warn(result)
                end

                return isPartOwner(part)
            end

            local function findNicePart(model)
                if not model or not model:IsA("Model") then
                    return
                end
                local cached = grabbableParts[model]
                if cached and cached:IsDescendantOf(model) and cached.CanQuery and not cached.Anchored then
                    return cached
                end
                grabbableParts[model] = nil
                local fallback = nil

                for _, part in ipairs(model:QueryDescendants("BasePart")) do
                    if part.CanQuery and not part.Anchored and part.Name ~= "Button" then
                        fallback = fallback or part
                        if (part.AssemblyRootPart or part) == part then
                            grabbableParts[model] = part
                            return part
                        end
                    end
                end

                grabbableParts[model] = fallback
                return fallback
            end

            local function pivotTo(model, cf, parts, offsets)
                if not model or not model:IsA("Model") or typeof(cf) ~= "CFrame" then
                    return false
                end
                local delta = cf * model:GetPivot():Inverse()
                parts = parts or model:QueryDescendants("BasePart")
                offsets = offsets or {}
                table.clear(offsets)

                for i, part in ipairs(parts) do
                    local rootPart = part:IsDescendantOf(model) and part.AssemblyRootPart

                    if rootPart and not part.Anchored and not rootPart.Anchored then
                        offsets[i] = delta * part.CFrame
                    end
                end

                for index, newCFrame in pairs(offsets) do
                    parts[index].CFrame = newCFrame
                end

                return true
            end

            local function destroyObject(model)
                if not model or not model:IsA("Model") then
                    return
                end
                local nicePart = findNicePart(model)
                if not nicePart or nicePart.Anchored or not takeOwnership(nicePart) then
                    return
                end

                workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = voidHeight
                pivotTo(model, CFrame.new(0, -11111, 0))

                if destroyThread then
                    task.cancel(destroyThread)
                end

                destroyThread = getgenv().__DSTH(task.delay(math.clamp(localPlayer:GetNetworkPing() + 0.05, 0.08, 0.25), function()
                    workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -10000
                    task.wait()
                    workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -100
                    destroyThread = nil
                end))

                local releasePart = nicePart
                getgenv().__DSTH(task.delay(0.5, function()
                    destroyGrabLine:FireServer(releasePart)
                end))

                return true
            end

            local function collectInventories()
                local inventories = {}

                local inOwnedPlot = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("InOwnedPlot")
                if inOwnedPlot and inOwnedPlot.Value then
                    local plotItems = workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
                    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")

                    if plotItems and plots then
                        for _, plot in pairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
                            local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
                            local owners = sign and sign:FindFirstChild("ThisPlotsOwners")

                            if owners then
                                for _, owner in pairs(owners:GetChildren()) do
                                    if owner:IsA("StringValue") and owner.Value == localPlayer.Name then
                                        local items = plotItems:FindFirstChild(plot.Name)
                                        if items then
                                            table.insert(inventories, items)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                local function addFolder(name)
                    local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild(name)
                    if folder and not folder.Name:find(localPlayer.Name) then
                        table.insert(inventories, folder)
                    end
                end

                for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    addFolder(player.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                end

                for _, name in ipairs({
                    "Balloons",
                    "Cave",
                    "Cubes",
                    "Drawers",
                    "Farm",
                    "Food",
                    "Furniture",
                    "Glass Boxes",
                    "Ladders",
                    "Large Animals",
                    "Lincoln Planks",
                    "Music",
                    "NOT New",
                    "Other",
                    "Pallets",
                    "Robloxians",
                    "Rollers",
                }) do
                    addFolder(name)
                end

                return inventories
            end

            local ok, err = pcall(function()
                local models = {}

                for _, inventory in pairs(collectInventories()) do
                    for _, child in pairs(inventory:GetChildren()) do
                        if child:IsA("Model") then
                            models[#models + 1] = child
                        end
                    end
                end

                notify("DShub", "Destroying " .. #models .. " toys", 3)

                for _, model in ipairs(models) do
                    destroyObject(model)
                end

                while destroyThread do
                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                end

                notify("DShub", "Destroyed " .. #models .. "/" .. #models .. " toys", 3)
            end)

            pcall(function()
                workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = originalHeight
            end)

            env.DestroyAllToysRunning = false

            if not ok then
                warn(err)
            end
        end))
        return
    end

        local function _applyGrabDistance()
            local _dist = getgenv().ReachTargetDistance
            local _gpEvents = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GamepassEvents")
            local _reachEvent = _gpEvents and _gpEvents:FindFirstChild("FurtherReachBoughtNotifier")
            if not _reachEvent then return end
            local _ok, _conns = pcall(getconnections, _reachEvent.OnClientEvent)
            if not _ok or type(_conns) ~= "table" then return end
            for _, _conn in ipairs(_conns) do
                local _func = _conn.Function
                if type(_func) == "function" and islclosure(_func) then
                    local _info = debug.getinfo(_func)
                    if _info.source and string.find(_info.source, "GrabbingScript") then
                        for _i, _value in pairs((debug.getupvalues(_func) :: any)) do
                            if type(_value) == "number" and (_value == 20 or _value == 30 or _value == 42) then
                                debug.setupvalue(_func, _i, _dist)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        _applyGrabDistance()

        getgenv().GrabDistanceConnection = getgenv().__DST(Players.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                task.wait(0.3)
                pcall(_applyGrabDistance)
                task.wait(0.7)
                pcall(_applyGrabDistance)
                task.wait(1.5)
                pcall(_applyGrabDistance)
            end))
        end))

    elseif lowerMsg == ".bring -all" then
        getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
            local rs_ba = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

            local function collectCharPartsBA(player)
                local char = player.Character
                if not char then return {} end
                local parts, hrp, head = {}, char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                if hrp then table.insert(parts, hrp) end
                if head then table.insert(parts, head) end
                for _, pName in ipairs({"Torso", "Left Arm", "Right Arm", "Left Leg", "Right Leg",
                                        "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "LeftUpperArm", "RightUpperArm",
                                        "LeftLowerArm", "RightLowerArm", "LeftUpperLeg", "RightUpperLeg",
                                        "LeftLowerLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "LeftFoot", "RightFoot",
                                        "LeftHand", "RightHand"}) do
                    local pt = char:FindFirstChild(pName)
                    if pt then table.insert(parts, pt) end
                end
                return parts
            end

            local function fastBringOneBA(targetPlayer, originCF)
                local tgtChar = targetPlayer.Character
                if not tgtChar then return end
                local myChar = lp.Character
                local myHrp = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not myHrp then return end
                local charParts, thrp =
                    collectCharPartsBA(targetPlayer),
                    tgtChar:FindFirstChild("Torso") or tgtChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if thrp then
                    bringFakeTP(function()
                        local tc = targetPlayer.Character
                        return tc and (tc:FindFirstChild("Torso") or tc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
                    end, true, CFrame.new(0, 5, 0))
                end
                local ownerLead = M.newOwnerLead()
                local deadline = tick() + 0.15
                while tick() < deadline do
                    local tc = targetPlayer.Character
                    if not tc then break end
                    for _, part in ipairs(charParts) do
                        if part and part.Parent then
                            pcall(function()
                                rs_ba.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(part, ownerLead(part))
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end
                bringFakeTP(nil, false)
                local tc2 = targetPlayer.Character
                if tc2 then
                    local head2 = tc2:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    local ownerTag = head2 and head2:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                    if ownerTag and ownerTag:IsA("StringValue") and ownerTag.Value == lp.Name then
                        for _, part in ipairs(collectCharPartsBA(targetPlayer)) do
                            if part and part.Parent then
                                pcall(function()
                                    part.CFrame = originCF * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            local allTargetsBA = {}
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p ~= lp and not lp:IsFriendsWith(p.UserId) then
                    table.insert(allTargetsBA, p)
                end
            end

            if #allTargetsBA == 0 then
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = "[ Bring -all ]",
                    Text = "대상 플레이어가 없습니다. (친구 제외)",
                    Duration = 3
                })
                return
            end

            local myCharBA = lp.Character
            local myHrpBA = myCharBA and myCharBA:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not myHrpBA then
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = "[ Bring -all ]",
                    Text = "캐릭터를 찾을 수 없습니다.",
                    Duration = 3
                })
                return
            end
            local gatherCFBA = myHrpBA.CFrame

            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "[ Bring -all 시작 ]",
                Text = "총 " .. #allTargetsBA .. "명 한 바퀴 Bring 시작!",
                Duration = 4
            })

            local skipped = {}
            for _, target in ipairs(allTargetsBA) do
                if isTargetInPlot(target) then
                    table.insert(skipped, target.Name)
                else
                    fastBringOneBA(target, gatherCFBA)
                end
            end

            local skipMsg = #skipped > 0 and (" | Plot 스킵: " .. table.concat(skipped, ", ")) or ""
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "[ Bring -all 완료 ]",
                Text = #allTargetsBA .. "명 한 바퀴 완료" .. skipMsg,
                Duration = 5
            })
        end))

    elseif lowerMsg:sub(1, 7) == ".bring " then
        local argStr = lowerMsg:sub(8):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        if argStr == "" then
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "[ 오류 ]",
                Text = ".bring [이름] 형식으로 입력하세요.",
                Duration = 3
            })
        else
            local hasComma = argStr:find(",") ~= nil

            local function findPlayer(namePart)
                for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= lp then
                        local pName, pDisplay = p.Name:lower(), p.DisplayName:lower()
                        if pName:sub(1, #namePart) == namePart or pDisplay:sub(1, #namePart) == namePart then
                            return p
                        end
                    end
                end
                return nil
            end

            local function doBringToMe(targetPlayer)
                local inPlot, plotItems = false, Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
                local playersInPlotsFolder = plotItems and plotItems:FindFirstChild("PlayersInPlots")
                if playersInPlotsFolder and playersInPlotsFolder:FindFirstChild(targetPlayer.Name) then
                    inPlot = true
                end
                if not inPlot then
                    local mySpawnFolder = Workspace:FindFirstChild(lp.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                    if mySpawnFolder then
                        local fChar = targetPlayer.Character
                        if fChar and fChar:IsDescendantOf(mySpawnFolder) then
                            inPlot = true
                        end
                    end
                end

                if inPlot then
                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                        Title = "[ Bring 불가 ]",
                        Text = targetPlayer.Name .. "님은 Plot 안에 있습니다.",
                        Duration = 4
                    })
                    return
                end

                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                    local rs_bring = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

                    local function getBringCharParts(player)
                        local char = player.Character
                        if not char then return nil end
                        local hrp, head = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                        return char, hrp, head
                    end

                    local tgtChar, tgtHrp, tgtHead = getBringCharParts(targetPlayer)
                    if not tgtHrp or not tgtHead then
                        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                            Title = "[ Bring 실패 ]",
                            Text = targetPlayer.Name .. "님의 캐릭터를 찾을 수 없습니다.",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                        return
                    end

                    local myChar = lp.Character
                    local myHrp, myHum =
                        myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"),
                        myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if not myHrp or not myHum or myHum.Health <= 0 then
                        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                            Title = "[ Bring 실패 ]",
                            Text = "내 캐릭터 상태가 올바르지 않습니다.",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                        return
                    end

                    local originCF = myHrp.CFrame

                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                        Title = "[ Bring ]",
                        Text = targetPlayer.Name .. "님에게 Bring을 시작합니다...",
                        Duration = 3
                    })

                    local tpRunning, bringSuccess, timeout, startTick = true, false, 3, tick()

                    bringFakeTP(function()
                        local tc = targetPlayer.Character
                        return tc and tc:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                    end, true, CFrame.new(0, 7, 0))

                    local ownerLead = M.newOwnerLead()
                    while tpRunning and tick() - startTick < timeout do
                        local mc2 = lp.Character
                        local mh2 = mc2 and mc2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if not mc2 or not mc2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or (mh2 and mh2.Health <= 0) then
                            break
                        end
                        local tc2 = targetPlayer.Character
                        local th2 = tc2 and tc2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if not tc2 or not tc2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or (th2 and th2.Health <= 0) then
                            break
                        end

                        local tgtTorso = tgtChar:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                        if tgtTorso then
                            rs_bring.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(tgtTorso, ownerLead(tgtTorso))
                        end

                        local ownerTag = tgtHead:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                        if ownerTag and ownerTag:IsA("StringValue") and ownerTag.Value == lp.Name then
                            bringSuccess = true
                            break
                        end

                        task.wait()
                    end

                    tpRunning = false
                    bringFakeTP(nil, false)
                    task.wait(0.05)

                    local finalMyHrp = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if finalMyHrp then
                        finalMyHrp.CFrame = originCF
                        finalMyHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        finalMyHrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end

                    if bringSuccess then
                        local fm = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if fm then
                            local targetCF = fm.CFrame
                            local freezeStart = tick()
                            while (tick() - freezeStart) < 1.3 do
                                if not tgtChar or not tgtChar.Parent then break end

                                local ownerTag = tgtHead and tgtHead:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                                if not ownerTag or not ownerTag:IsA("StringValue") or ownerTag.Value ~= lp.Name then
                                    break
                                end

                                local tgtTorso = tgtChar:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                                if tgtTorso then
                                    tgtTorso.CFrame = targetCF
                                    tgtTorso.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                    tgtTorso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                end
                                task.wait()
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    if tgtChar and tgtChar:FindFirstChild("Torso") then
                        local torso = tgtChar.Torso
                        rs_bring.GrabEvents.DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(torso)
                    end

                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                        Title = bringSuccess and "[ Bring 완료 ]" or "[ Bring 종료 ]",
                        Text = bringSuccess
                            and (targetPlayer.Name .. "님을 데려왔습니다.")
                            or (targetPlayer.Name .. "님의 소유권 획득에 실패했습니다."),
                        Duration = 4
                    })
                end))
            end

            local PRESET_POSITIONS = {
                ["-prison"]  = Vector3.new(566.54, 82.65, 207.35),
                ["-prison2"] = Vector3.new(518.63, 67.65, -166.72),
                ["-prison3"] = Vector3.new(-386.68, -7.35, 335.58),
                ["-barn"]    = Vector3.new(-229.78, 60.76, -319.97),
            }

            local function doBringToPosition(targetPlayer, destPos, destLabel)
                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                    local rs_bring, fromChar = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"), targetPlayer.Character
                    local fromHrp, fromHead =
                        fromChar and fromChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"),
                        fromChar and fromChar:FindFirstChild("Head")

                    if not fromHrp or not fromHead then
                        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                            Title = "[ Bring 실패 ]",
                            Text = targetPlayer.Name .. "님의 캐릭터를 찾을 수 없습니다.",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                        return
                    end

                    local myChar = lp.Character
                    local myHrp, myHum =
                        myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"),
                        myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if not myHrp or not myHum or myHum.Health <= 0 then
                        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                            Title = "[ Bring 실패 ]",
                            Text = "내 캐릭터 상태가 올바르지 않습니다.",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                        return
                    end

                    local originCF, destCF = myHrp.CFrame, CFrame.new(destPos)

                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                        Title = "[ Bring ]",
                        Text = targetPlayer.Name .. "님을 " .. destLabel .. "로 데려갑니다...",
                        Duration = 3
                    })

                    local tpRunning, bringSuccess, timeout, startTick = true, false, 3, tick()

                    bringFakeTP(function()
                        local fc = targetPlayer.Character
                        return fc and fc:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                    end, true, CFrame.new(0, 7, 0))

                    local ownerLead = M.newOwnerLead()
                    while tpRunning and tick() - startTick < timeout do
                        local mc2 = lp.Character
                        local mh2 = mc2 and mc2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if not mc2 or not mc2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or (mh2 and mh2.Health <= 0) then break end
                        local fc2 = targetPlayer.Character
                        local fh2 = fc2 and fc2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if not fc2 or not fc2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or (fh2 and fh2.Health <= 0) then break end

                        local fromTorso = fromChar:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                        if fromTorso then
                            rs_bring.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(fromTorso, ownerLead(fromTorso))
                        end

                        local ownerTag = fromHead:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                        if ownerTag and ownerTag:IsA("StringValue") and ownerTag.Value == lp.Name then
                            bringSuccess = true
                            break
                        end

                        task.wait()
                    end

                    tpRunning = false
                    bringFakeTP(nil, false)
                    task.wait(0.05)

                    local finalMyHrp = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if finalMyHrp then
                        finalMyHrp.CFrame = originCF
                        finalMyHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        finalMyHrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end

                    if bringSuccess then
                        local freezeStart = tick()
                        while (tick() - freezeStart) < 1.3 do
                            if not fromChar or not fromChar.Parent then break end

                            local ownerTag = fromHead and fromHead:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                            if not ownerTag or not ownerTag:IsA("StringValue") or ownerTag.Value ~= lp.Name then
                                break
                            end

                            local fromTorso = fromChar:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                            if fromTorso then
                                fromTorso.CFrame = destCF
                                fromTorso.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                fromTorso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            end
                            task.wait()
                        end
                    end

                    if fromChar and fromChar:FindFirstChild("Torso") then
                        local torso = fromChar.Torso
                        rs_bring.GrabEvents.DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(torso)
                    end

                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                        Title = bringSuccess and "[ Bring 완료 ]" or "[ Bring 종료 ]",
                        Text = bringSuccess
                            and (targetPlayer.Name .. "님을 " .. destLabel .. "로 데려갔습니다.")
                            or (targetPlayer.Name .. "님의 소유권 획득에 실패했습니다."),
                        Duration = 4
                    })
                end))
            end

            if not hasComma then
                local parts = {}
                for token in argStr:gmatch("%S+") do
                    table.insert(parts, token)
                end

                if #parts == 2 and PRESET_POSITIONS[parts[2]] then
                    local targetName, presetKey = parts[1], parts[2]
                    local targetPlayer = findPlayer(targetName)
                    if not targetPlayer then
                        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                            Title = "[ 오류 ]",
                            Text = "'" .. targetName .. "' 에 해당하는 플레이어를 찾을 수 없습니다.",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                    else
                        doBringToPosition(targetPlayer, PRESET_POSITIONS[presetKey], presetKey)
                    end

                elseif #parts >= 2 then
                    local coordStr = table.concat(parts, " ", 2)
                    local cx, cy, cz = coordStr:match("^(-?%d+%.?%d*)%s*,%s*(-?%d+%.?%d*)%s*,%s*(-?%d+%.?%d*)$")
                    if cx and cy and cz then
                        local targetName   = parts[1]
                        local targetPlayer = findPlayer(targetName)
                        if not targetPlayer then
                            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                Title = "[ 오류 ]",
                                Text = "'" .. targetName .. "' 에 해당하는 플레이어를 찾을 수 없습니다.",
                                Duration = 3
                            })
                        else
                            local destPos, destLabel =
                                Vector3.new(tonumber(cx), tonumber(cy), tonumber(cz)),
                                string.format("(%.2f, %.2f, %.2f)", (tonumber(cx) :: number), (tonumber(cy) :: number), (tonumber(cz) :: number))
                            doBringToPosition(targetPlayer, destPos, destLabel)
                        end

                    elseif #parts == 2 then
                        local fromName, toName = parts[1], parts[2]
                        local fromPlayer, toPlayer = findPlayer(fromName), findPlayer(toName)

                        if not fromPlayer then
                            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                Title = "[ 오류 ]",
                                Text = "'" .. fromName .. "' 에 해당하는 플레이어를 찾을 수 없습니다.",
                                Duration = 3
                            })
                        elseif not toPlayer then
                            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                Title = "[ 오류 ]",
                                Text = "'" .. toName .. "' 에 해당하는 플레이어를 찾을 수 없습니다.",
                                Duration = 3
                            })
                        else
                        getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                            local rs_bring, fromChar = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"), fromPlayer.Character
                            local fromHrp, fromHead, toChar =
                                fromChar and fromChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"),
                                fromChar and fromChar:FindFirstChild("Head"),
                                toPlayer.Character
                            local toHrp  = toChar and toChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                            if not fromHrp or not fromHead then
                                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                    Title = "[ Bring 실패 ]",
                                    Text = fromPlayer.Name .. "님의 캐릭터를 찾을 수 없습니다.",
                                    Duration = 3
                                })
                                return
                            end
                            if not toHrp then
                                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                    Title = "[ Bring 실패 ]",
                                    Text = toPlayer.Name .. "님의 캐릭터를 찾을 수 없습니다.",
                                    Duration = 3
                                })
                                return
                            end

                            local myChar = lp.Character
                            local myHrp, myHum =
                                myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"),
                                myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if not myHrp or not myHum or myHum.Health <= 0 then
                                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                    Title = "[ Bring 실패 ]",
                                    Text = "내 캐릭터 상태가 올바르지 않습니다.",
                                    Duration = 3
                                })
                                return
                            end

                            local originCF, destCF = myHrp.CFrame, toHrp.CFrame

                            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                Title = "[ Bring ]",
                                Text = fromPlayer.Name .. "님을 " .. toPlayer.Name .. "님에게 데려갑니다...",
                                Duration = 3
                            })

                            local tpRunning, bringSuccess, timeout, startTick = true, false, 3, tick()

                            bringFakeTP(function()
                                local fc = fromPlayer.Character
                                return fc and fc:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                            end, true, CFrame.new(0, 7, 0))

                            local ownerLead = M.newOwnerLead()
                            while tpRunning and tick() - startTick < timeout do
                                local mc2 = lp.Character
                                local mh2 = mc2 and mc2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                if not mc2 or not mc2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or (mh2 and mh2.Health <= 0) then break end
                                local fc2 = fromPlayer.Character
                                local fh2 = fc2 and fc2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                if not fc2 or not fc2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or (fh2 and fh2.Health <= 0) then break end

                                local fromTorso = fromChar:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                                if fromTorso then
                                    rs_bring.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(fromTorso, ownerLead(fromTorso))
                                end

                                local ownerTag = fromHead:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                                if ownerTag and ownerTag:IsA("StringValue") and ownerTag.Value == lp.Name then
                                    bringSuccess = true
                                    break
                                end

                                task.wait()
                            end

                            tpRunning = false
                            bringFakeTP(nil, false)
                            task.wait(0.05)

                            local finalMyHrp = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if finalMyHrp then
                                finalMyHrp.CFrame = originCF
                                finalMyHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                finalMyHrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            end

                            if bringSuccess then
                                local freezeStart = tick()
                                while (tick() - freezeStart) < 1.3 do
                                    if not fromChar or not fromChar.Parent then break end

                                    local ownerTag = fromHead and fromHead:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                                    if not ownerTag or not ownerTag:IsA("StringValue") or ownerTag.Value ~= lp.Name then
                                        break
                                    end

                                    local currentToHrp = toPlayer.Character and toPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    local targetCF = currentToHrp and (currentToHrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(2, 0, 0)) or destCF
                                    local fromTorso = fromChar:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                                    if fromTorso then
                                        fromTorso.CFrame = targetCF
                                        fromTorso.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                        fromTorso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                    end
                                    task.wait()
                                end
                            end

                            if fromChar and fromChar:FindFirstChild("Torso") then
                                local torso = fromChar.Torso
                                rs_bring.GrabEvents.DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(torso)
                            end

                            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                Title = bringSuccess and "[ Bring 완료 ]" or "[ Bring 종료 ]",
                                Text = bringSuccess
                                    and (fromPlayer.Name .. "님을 " .. toPlayer.Name .. "님에게 데려갔습니다.")
                                    or (fromPlayer.Name .. "님의 소유권 획득에 실패했습니다."),
                                Duration = 4
                            })
                        end))
                        end
                    else
                        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                            Title = "[ 오류 ]",
                            Text = ".bring 사용법: .bring [이름] / .bring [타겟] [이름] / .bring [타겟] -prison / .bring [타겟] X,Y,Z",
                            Duration = 4
                        })
                    end
                else
                    local targetNamePart = argStr
                    local foundPlayer = findPlayer(targetNamePart)
                    if not foundPlayer then
                        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                            Title = "[ 오류 ]",
                            Text = "'" .. targetNamePart .. "' 에 해당하는 플레이어를 찾을 수 없습니다.",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                    else
                        doBringToMe(foundPlayer)
                    end
                end
            else
                local names = {}
                for name in argStr:gmatch("[^,]+") do
                    local trimmed = name:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
                    if trimmed ~= "" then table.insert(names, trimmed) end
                end
                for _, namePart in ipairs(names) do
                    local foundPlayer = findPlayer(namePart)
                    if not foundPlayer then
                        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                            Title = "[ 오류 ]",
                            Text = "'" .. namePart .. "' 에 해당하는 플레이어를 찾을 수 없습니다.",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                    else
                        doBringToMe(foundPlayer)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

    elseif lowerMsg == ".lpbring -all" or lowerMsg == ".loopbring -all" then
        stopLpBring()

        getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
            local rs_lb = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

            local function fastBringOne(targetPlayer, destCF)
                local tgtChar = targetPlayer.Character
                if not tgtChar then return end
                local myChar = lp.Character
                local myHrp = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not myHrp then return end

                local tgtTorso = tgtChar:FindFirstChild("Torso") or tgtChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not tgtTorso then return end

                bringFakeTP(function()
                    local tc = targetPlayer.Character
                    return tc and (tc:FindFirstChild("Torso") or tc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
                end, true, CFrame.new(0, 5, 0))

                local ownerLead = M.newOwnerLead()
                local deadline = tick() + 0.15
                local bringSuccess = false
                while tick() < deadline do
                    local tc = targetPlayer.Character
                    if not tc then break end
                    local torso = tc:FindFirstChild("Torso") or tc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if torso then
                        pcall(function()
                            rs_lb.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(torso, ownerLead(torso))
                        end)
                    end

                    local tgtHead = tc:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    local ownerTag = tgtHead and tgtHead:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                    if ownerTag and ownerTag:IsA("StringValue") and ownerTag.Value == lp.Name then
                        bringSuccess = true
                        break
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end

                bringFakeTP(nil, false)
                task.wait(0.05)

                local tc2 = targetPlayer.Character
                if tc2 and bringSuccess then
                    local tgtHead2 = tc2:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    local torso2 = tc2:FindFirstChild("Torso") or tc2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local freezeStart = tick()

                    while (tick() - freezeStart) < 1.3 do
                        if not targetPlayer.Character or targetPlayer.Character ~= tc2 then break end

                        local ownerTag2 = tgtHead2 and tgtHead2:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                        if not ownerTag2 or not ownerTag2:IsA("StringValue") or ownerTag2.Value ~= lp.Name then
                            break
                        end

                        if torso2 and torso2.Parent then
                            pcall(function()
                                torso2.CFrame = destCF * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)
                                torso2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                torso2.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            end)
                        end
                        task.wait()
                    end

                    if torso2 and torso2.Parent then
                        pcall(function()
                            rs_lb.GrabEvents.DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(torso2)
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end

            local allTargets = {}
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p ~= lp and not lp:IsFriendsWith(p.UserId) then
                    table.insert(allTargets, p)
                end
            end

            if #allTargets == 0 then
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = "[ LpBring -all ]",
                    Text = "대상 플레이어가 없습니다. (친구 제외)",
                    Duration = 3
                })
                return
            end

            local myCharInit = lp.Character
            local myHrpInit = myCharInit and myCharInit:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not myHrpInit then
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = "[ LpBring -all ]",
                    Text = "캐릭터를 찾을 수 없습니다.",
                    Duration = 3
                })
                return
            end
            local gatherCF, initialTargets, inPlotNames = myHrpInit.CFrame, {}, {}
            for _, p in ipairs(allTargets) do
                if isTargetInPlot(p) then
                    table.insert(inPlotNames, p.Name)
                else
                    table.insert(initialTargets, p)
                end
            end

            L.lpBringEnabled = true
            L.lpBringTargets = allTargets

            if #inPlotNames > 0 then
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = "[ Plot 내 플레이어 감지 ]",
                    Text = table.concat(inPlotNames, ", ") .. "님은 Plot 안에 있어 나오면 자동 Bring합니다.",
                    Duration = 5
                })
            end

            local total = #initialTargets
            if total > 0 then
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = "[ LpBring -all 반복 시작 ]",
                    Text = "총 " .. total .. "명 반복 Bring 시작! (1초 간격으로 계속 반복)",
                    Duration = 4
                })
            else
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = "[ LpBring -all 대기 중 ]",
                    Text = "현재 모든 대상이 Plot 안에 있습니다. 나오면 자동 Bring합니다.",
                    Duration = 5
                })
            end

            local targetPaused, wasInPlot, _plotExitBringing, _plotNotifyCooldown = {}, {}, {}, {}

            for _, target in ipairs(L.lpBringTargets) do
                targetPaused[target] = false
                wasInPlot[target] = isTargetInPlot(target)
            end

            getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                while L.lpBringEnabled do
                    local currentTargets = {}
                    for _, t in ipairs(L.lpBringTargets) do
                        table.insert(currentTargets, t)
                    end

                    for _, target in ipairs(currentTargets) do
                        if not L.lpBringEnabled then break end
                        if not targetPaused[target] and not isTargetInPlot(target) then
                            local tgtChar = target.Character
                            local skipBring = false
                            if tgtChar then
                                local tgtHead = tgtChar:FindFirstChild("Head")
                                local ownerTag = tgtHead and tgtHead:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                                if ownerTag and ownerTag:IsA("StringValue") and ownerTag.Value == lp.Name then
                                    skipBring = true
                                end
                            end
                            if not skipBring then
                                fastBringOne(target, gatherCF)
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    if not L.lpBringEnabled then break end
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end
            end))

            local function setupDeathWatch(target)
                if L.lpBringDeathConns[target] then
                    pcall(function() L.lpBringDeathConns[target]:Disconnect() end)
                    L.lpBringDeathConns[target] = nil
                end
                local char = target.Character
                if not char then return end
                local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not hum then return end

                L.lpBringDeathConns[target] = getgenv().__DST(hum.Died:Connect(function()
                    if not L.lpBringEnabled then return end
                    targetPaused[target] = true
                    local respawnConn
                    respawnConn = getgenv().__DST(target.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
                        if not L.lpBringEnabled then respawnConn:Disconnect(); return end
                        getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                            local newHrp = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 3)
                            if not newHrp or not L.lpBringEnabled then
                                respawnConn:Disconnect()
                                return
                            end

                            local destCF, myChar = gatherCF, lp.Character
                            local myHrp = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if myHrp then
                                pcall(function() myHrp.CFrame = newHrp.CFrame - Vector3.new(0, 13, 0) end)
                            end

                            local deadline, acquired = tick() + 3, false
                            while tick() < deadline and L.lpBringEnabled do
                                if target.Character ~= newChar then break end

                                local head = newChar:FindFirstChild("Head")
                                local ownerTag = head and head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                                if ownerTag and ownerTag:IsA("StringValue") and ownerTag.Value == lp.Name then
                                    acquired = true
                                    break
                                end

                                local torso = newChar:FindFirstChild("Torso") or newChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if torso then
                                    pcall(function()
                                        rs_lb.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(torso, torso.CFrame)
                                    end)
                                end
                                task.wait()
                            end

                            if acquired and target.Character == newChar then
                                local freezeStart = tick()
                                local tgtHead = newChar:FindFirstChild("Head")
                                local torso = newChar:FindFirstChild("Torso") or newChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                while (tick() - freezeStart) < 1.3 do
                                    if target.Character ~= newChar then break end
                                    local ownerTag = tgtHead and tgtHead:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                                    if not ownerTag or not ownerTag:IsA("StringValue") or ownerTag.Value ~= lp.Name then
                                        break
                                    end
                                    if torso and torso.Parent then
                                        pcall(function()
                                            torso.CFrame = destCF * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)
                                            torso.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                            torso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                        end)
                                    end
                                    task.wait()
                                end
                                if torso and torso.Parent then
                                    pcall(function()
                                        rs_lb.GrabEvents.DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(torso)
                                    end)
                                end
                            end

                            targetPaused[target] = false
                            if not L.lpBringEnabled then respawnConn:Disconnect(); return end
                            setupDeathWatch(target)
                            respawnConn:Disconnect()
                        end))
                    end))
                end))
            end

            for _, target in ipairs(L.lpBringTargets) do
                targetPaused[target] = false
                setupDeathWatch(target)
            end

            local joinConn
            joinConn = getgenv().__DST(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(newPlayer)
                if not L.lpBringEnabled then joinConn:Disconnect(); return end
                task.wait(1)
                if lp:IsFriendsWith(newPlayer.UserId) then return end
                for _, t in ipairs(L.lpBringTargets) do
                    if t == newPlayer then return end
                end
                table.insert(L.lpBringTargets, newPlayer)
                targetPaused[newPlayer] = false
                wasInPlot[newPlayer] = isTargetInPlot(newPlayer)
                setupDeathWatch(newPlayer)
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = "[ LpBring -all ]",
                    Text = newPlayer.Name .. "님이 접속하여 타겟에 추가됩니다.",
                    Duration = 3
                })
            end))

            local leaveConn
            leaveConn = getgenv().__DST(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(leavingPlayer)
                if not L.lpBringEnabled then leaveConn:Disconnect(); return end
                for i, target in ipairs(L.lpBringTargets) do
                    if target == leavingPlayer then
                        table.remove(L.lpBringTargets, i)
                        targetPaused[leavingPlayer] = nil
                        wasInPlot[leavingPlayer] = nil
                        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                            Title = "[ LpBring -all ]",
                            Text = leavingPlayer.Name .. "님이 퇴장했습니다. 나머지 " .. #L.lpBringTargets .. "명 유지.",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                        break
                    end
                end
            end))

            local reBringing, _lastTpTime, lastSnoTime = {}, {}, {}
            L.lpBringLoop = getgenv().__DST(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not L.lpBringEnabled then return end
                local now = tick()
                for _, target in ipairs(L.lpBringTargets) do
                    if not L.lpBringEnabled then break end
                    repeat
                        if targetPaused[target] then break end

                        local inPlot = isTargetInPlot(target)
                        if inPlot then
                            wasInPlot[target] = true
                            break
                        else
                            if wasInPlot[target] then
                                wasInPlot[target] = false
                                if not reBringing[target] then
                                    reBringing[target] = true
                                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                        Title = "[ Plot 탈출 감지 ]",
                                        Text = target.Name .. "님이 Plot 밖으로 나왔습니다. 즉시 Bring!",
                                        Duration = 4
                                    })
                                    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                                        fastBringOne(target, gatherCF)
                                        reBringing[target] = false
                                    end))
                                end
                                break
                            end
                            wasInPlot[target] = false
                        end

                        local tgtChar = target.Character
                        if not tgtChar then break end
                        local tgtHrp, tgtHum =
                            tgtChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"),
                            tgtChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if not tgtHrp then break end
                        if tgtHum and tgtHum.Health <= 0 then
                            targetPaused[target] = true
                            break
                        end

                        local tgtHead = tgtChar:FindFirstChild("Head")
                        local ownerTag = tgtHead and tgtHead:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                        local hasOwnership = ownerTag and ownerTag:IsA("StringValue") and ownerTag.Value == lp.Name

                        local tgtTorso = tgtChar:FindFirstChild("Torso") or tgtChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                        if hasOwnership then
                            if tgtTorso then
                                pcall(function()
                                    tgtTorso.CFrame = gatherCF * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)
                                    tgtTorso.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                    tgtTorso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                end)
                            end
                        else
                            if not lastSnoTime[target] or (now - lastSnoTime[target]) >= 0.1 then
                                lastSnoTime[target] = now
                                if tgtTorso then
                                    pcall(function()
                                        rs_lb.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(tgtTorso, tgtTorso.CFrame)
                                    end)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    until true
                end
            end))
        end))

    elseif lowerMsg:sub(1, 9) == ".lpbring " or lowerMsg:sub(1, 11) == ".loopbring " then
        local argStart = lowerMsg:sub(1, 9) == ".lpbring " and 10 or 12
        local argRaw = msg:sub(argStart):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        local argRawLower = argRaw:lower()

        local LP_BRING_PRESET_POSITIONS = {
            ["-prison"]  = Vector3.new(566.54, 82.65, 207.35),
            ["-prison2"] = Vector3.new(518.63, 67.65, -166.72),
            ["-prison3"] = Vector3.new(-386.68, -7.35, 335.58),
            ["-barn"]    = Vector3.new(-229.78, 60.76, -319.97),
        }

        local lpBringPresetKey, lpBringPresetPos, lpBringNameArg = nil, nil, argRaw

        do
            local tokens = {}
            for token in argRawLower:gmatch("%S+") do table.insert(tokens, token) end
            if #tokens >= 2 then
                local lastToken = tokens[#tokens]
                if LP_BRING_PRESET_POSITIONS[lastToken] then
                    lpBringPresetKey = lastToken
                    lpBringPresetPos = LP_BRING_PRESET_POSITIONS[lastToken]
                    local nameEnd = #argRaw - #lastToken
                    lpBringNameArg = argRaw:sub(1, nameEnd):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
                end
            end
        end

        local names = {}
        if lpBringNameArg:find(",") then
            for part in lpBringNameArg:gmatch("[^,]+") do
                local trimmed = part:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
                if trimmed ~= "" then table.insert(names, trimmed:lower()) end
            end
        else
            local trimmed = lpBringNameArg:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
            if trimmed ~= "" then table.insert(names, trimmed:lower()) end
        end

        if #names == 0 then
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "[ 오류 ]",
                Text = ".lpbring [이름] 또는 .lpbring [이름] -prison/-prison2/-barn 형식으로 입력하세요.",
                Duration = 3
            })
        else
            local foundPlayers, notFound = {}, {}
            for _, namePart in ipairs(names) do
                local matched = nil
                for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= lp then
                        local pName, pDisplay = p.Name:lower(), p.DisplayName:lower()
                        if pName:sub(1, #namePart) == namePart or pDisplay:sub(1, #namePart) == namePart then
                            local alreadyAdded = false
                            for _, fp in ipairs(foundPlayers) do
                                if fp == p then alreadyAdded = true; break end
                            end
                            if not alreadyAdded then
                                matched = p
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if matched then
                    table.insert(foundPlayers, matched)
                else
                    table.insert(notFound, namePart)
                end
            end

            if #foundPlayers == 0 then
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = "[ 오류 ]",
                    Text = "일치하는 플레이어를 한 명도 찾을 수 없습니다.",
                    Duration = 3
                })
            elseif lpBringPresetPos then
                if #notFound > 0 then
                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                        Title = "[ 일부 미발견 ]",
                        Text = table.concat(notFound, ", ") .. " — 을(를) 찾지 못했습니다. 나머지 " .. #foundPlayers .. "명으로 진행합니다.",
                        Duration = 4
                    })
                end

                stopLpBring()

                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                    local rs_lb, PRESET_RANGE, presetDestPos, presetLabel =
                        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"),
                        20,
                        lpBringPresetPos,
                        lpBringPresetKey

                    local function collectCharParts(player)
                        local char = player.Character
                        if not char then return {} end
                        local parts, hrp, head =
                            {},
                            char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"),
                            char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                        if hrp then table.insert(parts, hrp) end
                        if head then table.insert(parts, head) end
                        for _, pName in ipairs({"Torso", "Left Arm", "Right Arm", "Left Leg", "Right Leg",
                                                "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "LeftUpperArm", "RightUpperArm",
                                                "LeftLowerArm", "RightLowerArm", "LeftUpperLeg", "RightUpperLeg",
                                                "LeftLowerLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "LeftFoot", "RightFoot",
                                                "LeftHand", "RightHand"}) do
                            local pt = char:FindFirstChild(pName)
                            if pt then table.insert(parts, pt) end
                        end
                        return parts
                    end

                    local function doBringOnce(targetPlayer)
                        local tgtChar = targetPlayer.Character
                        local tgtHrp, tgtHead =
                            tgtChar and tgtChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"),
                            tgtChar and tgtChar:FindFirstChild("Head")
                        if not tgtHrp or not tgtHead then return false end

                        local myChar = lp.Character
                        local myHrp, myHum =
                            myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"),
                            myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if not myHrp or not myHum or myHum.Health <= 0 then return false end

                        local _originCF, charParts, bringSuccess, timeout, startTick =
                            myHrp.CFrame,
                            collectCharParts(targetPlayer),
                            false,
                            4,
                            tick()

                        bringFakeTP(function()
                            local tc = targetPlayer.Character
                            return tc and (tc:FindFirstChild("Torso") or tc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
                        end, true, CFrame.new(0, 7, 0))

                        local ownerLead = M.newOwnerLead()
                        while tick() - startTick < timeout do
                            local mc2, tc2 = lp.Character, targetPlayer.Character
                            if not mc2 or not tc2 then break end
                            local mh3, th3 =
                                mc2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"),
                                tc2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if (mh3 and mh3.Health <= 0) or (th3 and th3.Health <= 0) then break end
                            for _, part in ipairs(charParts) do
                                if part and part.Parent then
                                    pcall(function() rs_lb.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(part, ownerLead(part)) end)
                                end
                            end
                            local ownerTag = tgtHead:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                            if ownerTag and ownerTag:IsA("StringValue") and ownerTag.Value == lp.Name then
                                bringSuccess = true
                                break
                            end
                            task.wait()
                        end

                        bringFakeTP(nil, false)

                    if bringSuccess then
                        local destCF, freshParts = CFrame.new(presetDestPos) * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0), collectCharParts(targetPlayer)
                        for _, part in ipairs(freshParts) do
                            if part and part.Parent then
                                pcall(function() part.CFrame = destCF end)
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    return bringSuccess
                    end

                    local function isTargetInRange(targetPlayer)
                        local tgtChar = targetPlayer.Character
                        local tgtHrp = tgtChar and tgtChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if not tgtHrp then return false end
                        return (tgtHrp.Position - presetDestPos).Magnitude <= PRESET_RANGE
                    end

                    local targetArrivedState, targetDeathConnsPreset = {}, {}

                    L.lpBringEnabled = true
                    L.lpBringTargets = foundPlayers

                    local function setupPresetDeathWatch(target)
                        if targetDeathConnsPreset[target] then
                            pcall(function() targetDeathConnsPreset[target]:Disconnect() end)
                            targetDeathConnsPreset[target] = nil
                        end

                        targetDeathConnsPreset[target] = getgenv().__DST(target.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
                            if not L.lpBringEnabled then return end
                            targetArrivedState[target] = false

                            getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                                local newHrp = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
                                if not newHrp or not L.lpBringEnabled then return end
                                if target.Character ~= newChar then return end

                                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                    Title = "[ LpBring " .. presetLabel .. " ]",
                                    Text = target.Name .. "님이 부활했습니다. " .. presetLabel .. " 으로 다시 데려갑니다...",
                                    Duration = 3
                                })

                                local ok = doBringOnce(target)

                                if ok then
                                    task.wait(0.15)
                                    if isTargetInRange(target) then
                                        targetArrivedState[target] = true
                                        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                            Title = "[ LpBring " .. presetLabel .. " ]",
                                            Text = target.Name .. "님을 " .. presetLabel .. " 으로 이동 완료.",
                                            Duration = 3
                                        })
                                    else
                                        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                            Title = "[ LpBring " .. presetLabel .. " ]",
                                            Text = target.Name .. "님이 범위 밖에 있습니다. 다시 시도합니다...",
                                            Duration = 3
                                        })
