                                        task.wait(0.15)
                                        local ok2 = doBringOnce(target)
                                        if ok2 then
                                            task.wait(0.15)
                                            if isTargetInRange(target) then
                                                targetArrivedState[target] = true
                                                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                                    Title = "[ LpBring " .. presetLabel .. " ]",
                                                    Text = target.Name .. "님을 " .. presetLabel .. " 으로 이동 완료.",
                                                    Duration = 3
                                                })
                                            else
                                                targetArrivedState[target] = false
                                                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                                    Title = "[ LpBring " .. presetLabel .. " ]",
                                                    Text = target.Name .. "님 이동 실패. 다음 부활 시 재시도.",
                                                    Duration = 4
                                                })
                                            end
                                        else
                                            targetArrivedState[target] = false
                                        end
                                    end
                                else
                                    targetArrivedState[target] = false
                                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                        Title = "[ LpBring " .. presetLabel .. " ]",
                                        Text = target.Name .. "님 소유권 획득 실패. 다음 부활 시 재시도.",
                                        Duration = 4
                                    })
                                end

                                if L.lpBringEnabled then
                                    setupPresetDeathWatch(target)
                                end
                            end))
                        end))
                    end

                    local total = #foundPlayers
                    for i, target in ipairs(foundPlayers) do
                        targetArrivedState[target] = false

                        if isTargetInPlot(target) then
                            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                Title = "[ Plot 내부 감지 | " .. i .. "/" .. total .. " ]",
                                Text = target.Name .. "님은 Plot 안에 있습니다. Bring 건너뜁니다.",
                                Duration = 3
                            })
                        else
                            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                Title = "[ LpBring " .. presetLabel .. " | " .. i .. "/" .. total .. " ]",
                                Text = target.Name .. "님을 " .. presetLabel .. " 으로 데려갑니다...",
                                Duration = 3
                            })

                            local ok = doBringOnce(target)

                            if ok then
                                task.wait(0.3)
                                if isTargetInRange(target) then
                                    targetArrivedState[target] = true
                                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                        Title = "[ LpBring " .. presetLabel .. " | " .. i .. "/" .. total .. " ]",
                                        Text = target.Name .. "님 이동 완료.",
                                        Duration = 3
                                    })
                                else
                                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                        Title = "[ LpBring " .. presetLabel .. " | " .. i .. "/" .. total .. " ]",
                                        Text = target.Name .. "님이 범위 밖에 있습니다. 다시 시도합니다...",
                                        Duration = 3
                                    })
                                    task.wait(0.3)
                                    local ok2 = doBringOnce(target)
                                    if ok2 then
                                        task.wait(0.3)
                                        if isTargetInRange(target) then
                                            targetArrivedState[target] = true
                                            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                                Title = "[ LpBring " .. presetLabel .. " | " .. i .. "/" .. total .. " ]",
                                                Text = target.Name .. "님 이동 완료.",
                                                Duration = 3
                                            })
                                        else
                                            targetArrivedState[target] = false
                                            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                                Title = "[ LpBring " .. presetLabel .. " | " .. i .. "/" .. total .. " ]",
                                                Text = target.Name .. "님 이동 실패. 다음 부활 시 재시도.",
                                                Duration = 4
                                            })
                                        end
                                    else
                                        targetArrivedState[target] = false
                                        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                            Title = "[ LpBring " .. presetLabel .. " | " .. i .. "/" .. total .. " ]",
                                            Text = target.Name .. "님 소유권 획득 실패. 다음 부활 시 재시도.",
                                            Duration = 4
                                        })
                                    end
                                end
                            else
                                targetArrivedState[target] = false
                                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                    Title = "[ LpBring " .. presetLabel .. " | " .. i .. "/" .. total .. " ]",
                                    Text = target.Name .. "님 소유권 획득 실패. 다음 부활 시 재시도.",
                                    Duration = 4
                                })
                            end

                            if i < total then task.wait(0.3) end
                        end

                        setupPresetDeathWatch(target)
                    end

                    L.lpBringDeathConns = targetDeathConnsPreset
                    L.lpBringLoop = true

                    local ESCAPE_RANGE, escapeRebringCooldown = 10, {}

                    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                        while L.lpBringEnabled do
                            task.wait(1)
                            if not L.lpBringEnabled then break end
                            for _, target in ipairs(L.lpBringTargets) do
                                if not L.lpBringEnabled then break end
                                if targetArrivedState[target] then
                                    local tgtChar = target.Character
                                    local tgtHrp = tgtChar and tgtChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    if tgtHrp then
                                        local dist = (tgtHrp.Position - presetDestPos).Magnitude
                                        if dist > ESCAPE_RANGE then
                                            local now = tick()
                                            if not escapeRebringCooldown[target] or now - escapeRebringCooldown[target] >= 3 then
                                                escapeRebringCooldown[target] = now
                                                targetArrivedState[target] = false
                                                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                                    Title = "[ LpBring " .. presetLabel .. " ]",
                                                    Text = target.Name .. "님이 " .. presetLabel .. " 에서 10 Studs 이상 벗어났습니다. 다시 이동합니다...",
                                                    Duration = 3
                                                })
                                                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                                                    local ok = doBringOnce(target)
                                                    if ok then
                                                        task.wait(0.3)
                                                        if isTargetInRange(target) then
                                                            targetArrivedState[target] = true
                                                        else
                                                            task.wait(0.3)
                                                            local ok2 = doBringOnce(target)
                                                            if ok2 then
                                                                task.wait(0.3)
                                                                targetArrivedState[target] = isTargetInRange(target)
                                                            end
                                                        end
                                                    end
                                                end))
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end))

                    local presetLeaveConn
                    presetLeaveConn = getgenv().__DST(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(leavingPlayer)
                        if not L.lpBringEnabled then presetLeaveConn:Disconnect(); return end
                        for i, target in ipairs(L.lpBringTargets) do
                            if target == leavingPlayer then
                                local leaveName = leavingPlayer.Name
                                table.remove(L.lpBringTargets, i)
                                if targetDeathConnsPreset[target] then
                                    pcall(function() targetDeathConnsPreset[target]:Disconnect() end)
                                    targetDeathConnsPreset[target] = nil
                                end
                                targetArrivedState[target] = nil
                                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                    Title = "[ LpBring " .. presetLabel .. " ]",
                                    Text = leaveName .. "님이 퇴장했습니다. 나머지 " .. #L.lpBringTargets .. "명 유지.",
                                    Duration = 4
                                })
                                break
                            end
                        end
                        if #L.lpBringTargets == 0 then
                            presetLeaveConn:Disconnect()
                        end
                    end))
                end))
            else
                if #notFound > 0 then
                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                        Title = "[ 일부 미발견 ]",
                        Text = table.concat(notFound, ", ") .. " — 을(를) 찾지 못했습니다. 나머지 " .. #foundPlayers .. "명으로 진행합니다.",
                        Duration = 4
                    })
                end

                local isAddMode = L.lpBringEnabled and L.lpBringLoop ~= nil

                if not isAddMode then
                    stopLpBring()
                end

                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                    local rs_lb = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

                    local function collectCharParts(player)
                        local char = player.Character
                        if not char then return {} end
                        local parts, hrp, head =
                            {},
                            char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"),
                            char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                        if hrp then table.insert(parts, hrp) end
                        if head then table.insert(parts, head) end
                        for _, pName in ipairs({"Torso", "Left Arm", "Right Arm", "Left Leg", "Right Leg",
                                                "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "LeftUpperArm", "RightUpperArm",
                                                "LeftLowerArm", "RightLowerArm", "LeftUpperLeg", "RightUpperLeg",
                                                "LeftLowerLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "LeftFoot", "RightFoot",
                                                "LeftHand", "RightHand"}) do
                            local pt = char:FindFirstChild(pName)
                            if pt then table.insert(parts, pt) end
                        end
                        return parts
                    end

                    local function doBring(targetPlayer)
                    if not targetPlayer or targetPlayer == lp then return false end
                    local tc = targetPlayer.Character
                    if not tc then return false end
                    local myHrp = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local originCF = myHrp and myHrp.CFrame
                    if not originCF then return false end

                    local bringSuccess = false
                    bringFakeTP(function()
                        local c = targetPlayer.Character
                        return c and (c:FindFirstChild("Torso") or c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
                    end, true, CFrame.new(0, 5, 0))

                    local charParts = collectCharParts(targetPlayer)
                    local ownerLead = M.newOwnerLead()
                    local deadline = tick() + 3
                    while tick() < deadline and L.lpBringEnabled do
                        if (getfenv() :: any).targetPaused[targetPlayer] then break end
                        local head = targetPlayer.Character and targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                        local ownerTag = head and head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                        if ownerTag and ownerTag:IsA("StringValue") and ownerTag.Value == lp.Name then
                            bringSuccess = true
                            break
                        end
                        for _, part in ipairs(charParts) do
                            if part and part.Parent then
                                pcall(function()
                                    rs_lb.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(part, ownerLead(part))
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                        task.wait()
                    end

                    bringFakeTP(nil, false)

                    if bringSuccess then
                        local fmHrp = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if fmHrp then
                            for _, part in ipairs(charParts) do
                                if part and part.Parent then
                                    pcall(function() part.CFrame = fmHrp.CFrame end)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    return bringSuccess
                end

                    local total, results = #foundPlayers, {}

                    for i, target in ipairs(foundPlayers) do
                        if isTargetInPlot(target) then
                            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                Title = "[ Plot 내부 감지 | " .. i .. "/" .. total .. " ]",
                                Text = target.Name .. "님은 Plot 안에 있습니다. Bring 건너뜁니다.",
                                Duration = 3
                            })
                            results[i] = "plot"
                        else
                            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                Title = "[ LpBring " .. i .. "/" .. total .. " ]",
                                Text = target.Name .. "님 Bring 중...",
                                Duration = 3
                            })

                            local ok = doBring(target)
                            results[i] = ok
                        end

                        if i < total then task.wait(0.3) end
                    end

                    local summaryParts = {}
                    for i, target in ipairs(foundPlayers) do
                        local r = results[i]
                        local icon = (r == "plot") and "" or (r and "" or "")
                        table.insert(summaryParts, target.Name .. icon)
                    end
                    local modeText = isAddMode and " — 기존 루프에 추가됨" or " — 네트워크 고정 루프 시작"
                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                        Title = "[ LpBring 완료 | " .. total .. "명 ]",
                        Text = table.concat(summaryParts, " / ") .. modeText,
                        Duration = 5
                    })

                    if isAddMode then
                        local existingAnchor = getgenv()._lpBringAnchorCF
                        for _, target in ipairs(foundPlayers) do
                            local alreadyIn = false
                            for _, t in ipairs(L.lpBringTargets) do
                                if t == target then alreadyIn = true; break end
                            end
                            if not alreadyIn then
                                table.insert(L.lpBringTargets, target)
                                if not getgenv()._lpBringPaused then getgenv()._lpBringPaused = {} end
                                if not getgenv()._lpBringFixedCF then getgenv()._lpBringFixedCF = {} end
                                getgenv()._lpBringPaused[target] = false
                                if existingAnchor then
                                    getgenv()._lpBringFixedCF[target] = existingAnchor
                                end
                                if getgenv()._lpBringSetupDeathWatch then getgenv()._lpBringSetupDeathWatch(target) end
                            end
                        end
                        local allNames = {}
                        for _, t in ipairs(L.lpBringTargets) do table.insert(allNames, t.Name:lower()) end
                        return
                    end

                    L.lpBringEnabled = true
                    L.lpBringTargets = foundPlayers

                    local savedNames = {}
                    for _, n in ipairs(names) do table.insert(savedNames, n) end

                    if not getgenv()._lpBringPaused then getgenv()._lpBringPaused = {} end
                    if not getgenv()._lpBringFixedCF then getgenv()._lpBringFixedCF = {} end
                    local targetPaused, targetFixedCF = getgenv()._lpBringPaused, getgenv()._lpBringFixedCF
                    for k in pairs(targetPaused) do targetPaused[k] = nil end
                    for k in pairs(targetFixedCF) do targetFixedCF[k] = nil end

                    local myHrpStart = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local anchorCF = myHrpStart and myHrpStart.CFrame
                    getgenv()._lpBringAnchorCF = anchorCF

                    for _, target in ipairs(foundPlayers) do
                        targetFixedCF[target] = anchorCF
                    end

                    local leaveConn
                    leaveConn = getgenv().__DST(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(leavingPlayer)
                        if not L.lpBringEnabled then
                            leaveConn:Disconnect()
                            return
                        end
                        for i, target in ipairs(L.lpBringTargets) do
                            if target == leavingPlayer then
                                local leaveName = leavingPlayer.Name
                                table.remove(L.lpBringTargets, i)
                                if L.lpBringDeathConns[target] then
                                    pcall(function() L.lpBringDeathConns[target]:Disconnect() end)
                                    L.lpBringDeathConns[target] = nil
                                end
                                targetPaused[target] = nil
                                targetFixedCF[target] = nil
                                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                    Title = "[ LpBring ]",
                                    Text = leaveName .. "님이 퇴장했습니다. 나머지 " .. #L.lpBringTargets .. "명 유지.",
                                    Duration = 4
                                })
                                if #L.lpBringTargets == 0 then
                                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                        Title = "[ LpBring 대기 중 ]",
                                        Text = "모든 타겟이 퇴장했습니다. 재입장 시 즉시 Bring 재개.",
                                        Duration = 5
                                    })
                                end
                                local rejoinConn
                                rejoinConn = getgenv().__DST(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(newPlayer)
                                    if newPlayer.Name ~= leaveName then return end
                                    rejoinConn:Disconnect()
                                    if not L.lpBringEnabled then return end
                                    task.wait(1)
                                    local alreadyIn = false
                                    for _, t in ipairs(L.lpBringTargets) do
                                        if t == newPlayer then alreadyIn = true; break end
                                    end
                                    if not alreadyIn then
                                        table.insert(L.lpBringTargets, newPlayer)
                                        targetPaused[newPlayer] = false
                                        local anchor = getgenv()._lpBringAnchorCF
                                        if anchor then
                                            targetFixedCF[newPlayer] = anchor
                                        end
                                        if getgenv()._lpBringSetupDeathWatch then
                                            getgenv()._lpBringSetupDeathWatch(newPlayer)
                                        end
                                        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                            Title = "[ LpBring 재입장 ]",
                                            Text = leaveName .. "님이 재입장했습니다. 즉시 Bring 시작!",
                                            Duration = 4
                                        })
                                        getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                                            local function doRejoinBring(charToUse)
                                                local myChar = lp.Character
                                                local myHrp = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                                local myOrigCF, ok = myHrp and myHrp.CFrame, doBring(newPlayer)
                                                if ok then
                                                    local dest = getgenv()._lpBringAnchorCF
                                                    if dest then
                                                        local tgtChar = newPlayer.Character
                                                        if tgtChar then
                                                            for _, part in ipairs(collectCharParts(newPlayer)) do
                                                                if part and part.Parent then
                                                                    pcall(function() part.CFrame = dest * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0) end)
                                                                end
                                                            end
                                                        end
                                                    end
                                                    local myHrpAfter = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                                    if myHrpAfter and myOrigCF then
                                                        pcall(function() myHrpAfter.CFrame = myOrigCF end)
                                                    end
                                                end
                                            end

                                            if newPlayer.Character then
                                                doRejoinBring(newPlayer.Character)
                                            end

                                            local rejoinCharConn
                                            rejoinCharConn = getgenv().__DST(newPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
                                                if not L.lpBringEnabled then
                                                    rejoinCharConn:Disconnect()
                                                    return
                                                end
                                                targetPaused[newPlayer] = true
                                                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                                                    local hrp = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
                                                    if not hrp or not L.lpBringEnabled then
                                                        targetPaused[newPlayer] = false
                                                        return
                                                    end
                                                    if newPlayer.Character ~= newChar then
                                                        targetPaused[newPlayer] = false
                                                        return
                                                    end
                                                    (getfenv() :: any).handleRespawnBring(newPlayer, newChar, nil)
                                                end))
                                            end))
                                            getgenv().__DSTH(task.delay(1, function()
                                                if rejoinCharConn then
                                                    pcall(function() rejoinCharConn:Disconnect() end)
                                                    rejoinCharConn = nil
                                                end
                                            end))
                                        end))
                                    end
                                end))
                                return
                            end
                        end
                    end))

                    local function handleRespawnBring(target, newChar, onComplete)
                        if not L.lpBringEnabled then return end
                        getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                            local newHrp = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 3)
                            if not newHrp or not L.lpBringEnabled then
                                targetPaused[target] = false
                                if onComplete then onComplete() end
                                return
                            end
                            if target.Character ~= newChar then
                                targetPaused[target] = false
                                if onComplete then onComplete() end
                                return
                            end

                            local destCF = getgenv()._lpBringAnchorCF or targetFixedCF[target] or (lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and lp.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
        
                            bringFakeTP(function()
                                return newChar and (newChar:FindFirstChild("Torso") or newChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
                            end, true, CFrame.new(0, 5, 0))

                            local ownerLead = M.newOwnerLead()
                            local parts, deadline, acquired = collectCharParts(target), tick() + 4, false
                            while tick() < deadline and L.lpBringEnabled do
                                if target.Character ~= newChar then break end
                                local head = newChar:FindFirstChild("Head")
                                local ownerTag = head and head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                                if ownerTag and ownerTag:IsA("StringValue") and ownerTag.Value == lp.Name then
                                    acquired = true
                                    break
                                end
                                for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
                                    if part and part.Parent then
                                        pcall(function() rs_lb.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(part, ownerLead(part)) end)
                                    end
                                end
                                task.wait()
                            end

                            bringFakeTP(nil, false)

                            if acquired and target.Character == newChar and destCF then
                                local freshParts = collectCharParts(target)
                                for _, part in ipairs(freshParts) do
                                    if part and part.Parent then
                                        pcall(function() part.CFrame = destCF * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0) end)
                                    end
                                end
                            end

                            if target.Character == newChar then
                                targetPaused[target] = false
                            end

                            if onComplete then onComplete() end
                        end))
                    end

                    local function setupDeathWatch(target)
                        if L.lpBringDeathConns[target] then
                            pcall(function() L.lpBringDeathConns[target]:Disconnect() end)
                            L.lpBringDeathConns[target] = nil
                        end

                        local _lastCharRef = target.Character

                        L.lpBringDeathConns[target] = getgenv().__DST(target.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
                            if not L.lpBringEnabled then return end
                            _lastCharRef = newChar
                            targetPaused[target] = true

                            getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                                local newHrp = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
                                if not newHrp or not L.lpBringEnabled then
                                    targetPaused[target] = false
                                    return
                                end
                                if target.Character ~= newChar then
                                    targetPaused[target] = false
                                    return
                                end

                                handleRespawnBring(target, newChar, function()
                                    if L.lpBringEnabled then
                                        setupDeathWatch(target)
                                    end
                                end)
                            end))
                        end))
                    end
                    getgenv()._lpBringSetupDeathWatch = setupDeathWatch
                    local function _setupDeathWatchGlobal(target)
                        if getgenv()._lpBringSetupDeathWatch then
                            getgenv()._lpBringSetupDeathWatch(target)
                        end
                    end

                    for _, target in ipairs(L.lpBringTargets) do
                        targetPaused[target] = false
                        setupDeathWatch(target)
                    end

                    local plotNotifyCooldown, wasInPlot, plotExitBringing = {}, {}, {}
                    if not getgenv()._lpBringOutOfRange then getgenv()._lpBringOutOfRange = {} end
                    local outOfRange, _lastTpTime, lastSnoTime = getgenv()._lpBringOutOfRange, {}, {}

                    L.lpBringLoop = getgenv().__DST(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                        if not L.lpBringEnabled then return end
                        local now = tick()
                        for _, target in ipairs(L.lpBringTargets) do
                          repeat
                            if targetPaused[target] then break end

                            local inPlot = isTargetInPlot(target)
                            if inPlot then
                                local t = tick()
                                if not plotNotifyCooldown[target] or (t - plotNotifyCooldown[target]) >= 15 then
                                    plotNotifyCooldown[target] = t
                                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                        Title = "[ Plot 내부 감지 ]",
                                        Text = target.Name .. "님은 Plot 안에 있습니다. Bring 건너뜀.",
                                        Duration = 4
                                    })
                                end
                                wasInPlot[target] = true
                                break
                            else
                                if wasInPlot[target] and not plotExitBringing[target] then
                                    plotExitBringing[target] = true
                                    plotNotifyCooldown[target] = nil
                                    wasInPlot[target] = false
                                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                        Title = "[ Plot 탈출 감지 ]",
                                        Text = target.Name .. "님이 Plot 밖으로 나왔습니다. 즉시 Bring!",
                                        Duration = 4
                                    })
                                    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                                        local destCF, ok =
                                            getgenv()._lpBringAnchorCF or targetFixedCF[target],
                                            doBring(target)
                                        if destCF then
                                            targetFixedCF[target] = destCF
                                            if ok then
                                                local tgtChar2 = target.Character
                                                if tgtChar2 then
                                                    for _, part in ipairs(collectCharParts(target)) do
                                                        if part and part.Parent then
                                                            pcall(function() part.CFrame = destCF * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0) end)
                                                        end
                                                    end
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                        plotExitBringing[target] = false
                                    end))
                                    break
                                end
                                wasInPlot[target] = false
                                plotNotifyCooldown[target] = nil
                            end

                            if target and target.Character then
                                local tgtChar = target.Character
                                local tgtHrp, tgtHum =
                                    tgtChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"),
                                    tgtChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                                if tgtHum and tgtHum.Health <= 0 then
                                    targetPaused[target] = true
                                    break
                                end

                                local tgtHead = tgtChar:FindFirstChild("Head")
                                local ownerTag = tgtHead and tgtHead:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                                local hasOwnership, fixedCF =
                                    ownerTag and ownerTag:IsA("StringValue") and ownerTag.Value == lp.Name,
                                    targetFixedCF[target]

                                if hasOwnership then
                                    if fixedCF and tgtHrp then
                                        local parts = collectCharParts(target)
                                        for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
                                            if part and part.Parent then
                                                pcall(function()
                                                    part.CFrame = fixedCF * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)
                                                end)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                else
                                    if not lastSnoTime[target] or (now - lastSnoTime[target]) >= 0.1 then
                                        lastSnoTime[target] = now
                                        local parts = collectCharParts(target)
                                        for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
                                            if part and part.Parent then
                                                pcall(function()
                                                    rs_lb.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(part, part.CFrame)
                                                end)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end

                                tgtHead = tgtChar:FindFirstChild("Head")
                                ownerTag = tgtHead and tgtHead:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                                hasOwnership = ownerTag and ownerTag:IsA("StringValue") and ownerTag.Value == lp.Name

                                if fixedCF and tgtHrp and not outOfRange[target] and not hasOwnership then
                                    outOfRange[target] = true
                                    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                                        local myChar = lp.Character
                                        local myHrp, curHrp =
                                            myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"),
                                            target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                                        local dist = (myHrp and curHrp and curHrp.Parent)
                                            and (myHrp.Position - curHrp.Position).Magnitude
                                            or math.huge

                                        local reAcquired = false

                                        if dist <= 25 then
                                            local reParts, deadline = collectCharParts(target), tick() + 3
                                            while tick() < deadline and L.lpBringEnabled do
                                                if targetPaused[target] then break end
                                                local head = target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                                                local ot = head and head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                                                if ot and ot:IsA("StringValue") and ot.Value == lp.Name then
                                                    reAcquired = true
                                                    break
                                                end
                                                for _, part in ipairs(reParts) do
                                                    if part and part.Parent then
                                                        pcall(function()
                                                            rs_lb.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(part, part.CFrame)
                                                        end)
                                                    end
                                                end
                                                task.wait()
                                            end

                                            if reAcquired then
                                                local freshParts, dest =
                                                    collectCharParts(target),
                                                    getgenv()._lpBringAnchorCF or fixedCF
                                                if dest then
                                                    for _, part in ipairs(freshParts) do
                                                        if part and part.Parent then
                                                            pcall(function() part.CFrame = dest * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0) end)
                                                        end
                                                    end
                                                end
                                            end
                                        else
                                            bringFakeTP(function()
                                                local tc = target.Character
                                                return tc and (tc:FindFirstChild("Torso") or tc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
                                            end, true, CFrame.new(0, 5, 0))

                                            local ownerLead = M.newOwnerLead()
                                            local reParts, deadline = collectCharParts(target), tick() + 3
                                            while tick() < deadline and L.lpBringEnabled do
                                                if targetPaused[target] then break end
                                                local head = target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                                                local ot = head and head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                                                if ot and ot:IsA("StringValue") and ot.Value == lp.Name then
                                                    reAcquired = true
                                                    break
                                                end
                                                for _, part in ipairs(reParts) do
                                                    if part and part.Parent then
                                                        pcall(function()
                                                            rs_lb.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(part, ownerLead(part))
                                                        end)
                                                    end
                                                end
                                                task.wait()
                                            end

                                            bringFakeTP(nil, false)

                                            if reAcquired then
                                                local freshParts, dest = collectCharParts(target), getgenv()._lpBringAnchorCF or fixedCF
                                                if dest then
                                                    for _, part in ipairs(freshParts) do
                                                        if part and part.Parent then
                                                            pcall(function() part.CFrame = dest * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0) end)
                                                        end
                                                    end
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end

                                        outOfRange[target] = false
                                    end))
                                end
                            end
                          until true
                        end
                    end))
                end))
            end
        end

    elseif lowerMsg == ".unlpbring" or lowerMsg == ".unloopbring" then
        if not L.lpBringEnabled then
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "[ LpBring ]",
                Text = "LpBring이 실행 중이 아닙니다.",
                Duration = 2
            })
        else
            stopLpBring(true)
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "[ LpBring 종료 ]",
                Text = "lpbring 중지 완료.",
                Duration = 3
            })
        end

    elseif lowerMsg:sub(1, 11) == ".unlpbring " or lowerMsg:sub(1, 13) == ".unloopbring " then
        if not L.lpBringEnabled then
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "[ LpBring ]",
                Text = "LpBring이 실행 중이 아닙니다.",
                Duration = 2
            })
        else
            local argStart2 = lowerMsg:sub(1, 11) == ".unlpbring " and 12 or 14
            local removeName = msg:sub(argStart2):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$"):lower()

            if removeName == "" then
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = "[ 오류 ]",
                    Text = ".unlpbring [이름] 형식으로 입력하세요.",
                    Duration = 3
                })
            else
                local removedTarget, removedIdx = nil, nil
                for i, target in ipairs(L.lpBringTargets) do
                    local pName, pDisplay = target.Name:lower(), target.DisplayName:lower()
                    if pName:sub(1, #removeName) == removeName or pDisplay:sub(1, #removeName) == removeName then
                        removedTarget = target
                        removedIdx = i
                        break
                    end
                end

                if not removedTarget then
                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                        Title = "[ 미발견 ]",
                        Text = removeName .. " — LpBring 목록에서 찾을 수 없습니다.",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                else
                    local removedName = removedTarget.Name
                    table.remove(L.lpBringTargets, removedIdx)
                    if L.lpBringDeathConns[removedTarget] then
                        pcall(function() L.lpBringDeathConns[removedTarget]:Disconnect() end)
                        L.lpBringDeathConns[removedTarget] = nil
                    end
                    if getgenv()._lpBringPaused then getgenv()._lpBringPaused[removedTarget] = nil end
                    if getgenv()._lpBringFixedCF then getgenv()._lpBringFixedCF[removedTarget] = nil end
                    if getgenv()._lpBringOutOfRange then getgenv()._lpBringOutOfRange[removedTarget] = nil end

                    if #L.lpBringTargets == 0 then
                        stopLpBring(true)
                        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                            Title = "[ LpBring 종료 ]",
                            Text = removedName .. "님 제거 후 남은 타겟이 없어 LpBring을 중지합니다.",
                            Duration = 4
                        })
                    else
                        local allNames = {}
                        for _, t in ipairs(L.lpBringTargets) do table.insert(allNames, t.Name:lower()) end
                        local remainNames = {}
                        for _, t in ipairs(L.lpBringTargets) do table.insert(remainNames, t.Name) end
                        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                            Title = "[ LpBring 타겟 제거 ]",
                            Text = removedName .. "님 제거됨. 남은 타겟: " .. table.concat(remainNames, ", ") .. " (" .. #L.lpBringTargets .. "명)",
                            Duration = 4
                        })
                    end
                end
            end
        end

    elseif lowerMsg == ".bringall" then
        (loadstring(game:HttpGet('https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dosi1100-cmyk/scripts/refs/heads/main/bringallfood%26Instrument')) :: () -> any)()
    elseif lowerMsg == ".unbringall" then
        local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")

        local function SendNotify(msg)
            pcall(function()
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = "[ 시스템 ]",
                    Text = msg,
                    Duration = 3
                })
            end)
        end

        if not _G.AntiBananaT then
            SendNotify("모든 아이템 금지 모드가 이미 꺼져 있습니다.")
            return
        end

        _G.AntiBananaT = false
        SendNotify("모든 아이템 금지 모드가 꺼졌습니다.")

    elseif lowerMsg:sub(1, 8) == ".pendik " then
        local args = {}
        for w in string.gmatch(msg:sub(9), "%S+") do
            table.insert(args, w)
        end

        local targetPlayer, TNUM = lp, nil

        if #args == 1 then
            local num = tonumber(args[1])
            if num then
                TNUM = num
                targetPlayer = lp
            else
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ 오류 ]", Text = "숫자를 입력해주세요. 예: .pendik 10", Duration = 3})
                return
            end
        elseif #args >= 2 then
            local namePart, num = args[1], tonumber(args[2])
            if not num then
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ 오류 ]", Text = "올바른 숫자를 입력하세요. 예: .pendik mir 10", Duration = 3})
                return
            end
            TNUM = num

            local found = nil
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p.Name:lower():sub(1, #namePart) == namePart:lower() or p.DisplayName:lower():sub(1, #namePart) == namePart:lower() then
                    found = p
                    break
                end
            end
            if found then
                targetPlayer = found
            else
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ 오류 ]", Text = "플레이어를 찾을 수 없습니다: " .. namePart, Duration = 3})
                return
            end
        end

        if not TNUM or TNUM <= 0 then return end

        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ Pendik 실행 ]", Text = targetPlayer.DisplayName .. "님에게 " .. TNUM .. "개 생성 시작...", Duration = 3})

        getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
            local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            local StickyPartEvent, SpawnToy, plr, targetChar =
                ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("PlayerEvents"):WaitForChild("StickyPartEvent"),
                ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("MenuToys"):WaitForChild("SpawnToyRemoteFunction"),
                lp,
                targetPlayer.Character or targetPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
            local targetTorso, myChar =
                targetChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart"):WaitForChild("RagdollTouchedHitbox"),
                lp.Character or lp.CharacterAdded:Wait()
            local myHRP = myChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)

            if not myHRP then
                warn("내 캐릭터 HRP를 찾을 수 없음")
                return
            end

            local function findFreshPencil()
                local myFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                if myFolder then
                    for _, child in pairs(myFolder:GetChildren()) do
                        if child.Name == "ToolPencil" and child:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") and child.StickyPart.Name == "StickyPart" then
                            return child
                        end
                    end
                end

                local plotItems = workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
                if plotItems then
                    for i = 1, 5 do
                        local pFolder = plotItems:FindFirstChild("Plot" .. i)
                        if pFolder then
                            for _, child in pairs(pFolder:GetChildren()) do
                                if child.Name == "ToolPencil" and child:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") and child.StickyPart.Name == "StickyPart" then
                                    return child
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return nil
            end

            local function createPencil(index)
                while not plr.CanSpawnToy.Value do task.wait() end

                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                    SpawnToy:InvokeServer("ToolPencil", myHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(0,5,15), Vector3.new(0,0,0))
                end))

                task.wait(0.3)

                local pencil = findFreshPencil()

                if not pencil then
                    print("Pencil not found in any inventory")
                    return nil
                end

                local stickyPart, SoundPart = pencil:WaitForChild("StickyPart"), pencil:WaitForChild("SoundPart")

                if stickyPart and SoundPart then
                    ReplicatedStorage.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(SoundPart, SoundPart.CFrame)
                    ReplicatedStorage.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(SoundPart, myHRP.CFrame)
                end

                local PartOwner = SoundPart:WaitForChild("PartOwner")
                if PartOwner then
                    SoundPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 599999, 999)
                end

                stickyPart.Name = "w" .. index

                for _, part in pairs(pencil:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Color == Color3.fromRGB(158, 108, 141) then
                        part.Name = "a" .. index
                    end
                end

                return pencil
            end

            local pencils = {}

            for i = 1, TNUM do
                local pencil = createPencil(i)
                if pencil then
                    table.insert(pencils, pencil)
                end
                task.wait(0.1)
            end

            for i = 1, #pencils - 1 do
                local currentPencil, nextPencil = pencils[i], pencils[i + 1]

                if currentPencil and nextPencil then
                    local attachFrom, attachTo =
                        currentPencil:FindFirstChild("w" .. i),
                        nextPencil:FindFirstChild("a" .. (i + 1))

                    if attachFrom and attachTo then
                        StickyPartEvent:FireServer(attachFrom, attachTo, CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(-90), 0))
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.05)
            end

            if targetTorso and #pencils >= TNUM then
                local lastPencil = pencils[TNUM]
                if lastPencil then
                    local wLast = lastPencil:FindFirstChild("w" .. TNUM)
                    if wLast then
                        local attachPosition = targetTorso.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -1.1, 0.1) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), 0)
                        local relativeCF = targetTorso.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(attachPosition)
                        StickyPartEvent:FireServer(wLast, targetTorso, relativeCF)
                    end
                end
            end
        end))

    elseif lowerMsg:sub(1, 10) == ".lppendik " then
        local args = {}
        for w in string.gmatch(msg:sub(11), "%S+") do
            table.insert(args, w)
        end

        local targetPlayer, TNUM = lp, nil

        if #args == 1 then
            local num = tonumber(args[1])
            if num then
                TNUM = num
                targetPlayer = lp
            else
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ 오류 ]", Text = "숫자를 입력해주세요. 예: .lppendik 3", Duration = 3})
                return
            end
        elseif #args >= 2 then
            local namePart, num = args[1], tonumber(args[2])
            if not num then
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ 오류 ]", Text = "올바른 숫자를 입력하세요. 예: .lppendik mir 3", Duration = 3})
                return
            end
            TNUM = num

            local found = nil
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p.Name:lower():sub(1, #namePart) == namePart:lower() or p.DisplayName:lower():sub(1, #namePart) == namePart:lower() then
                    found = p
                    break
                end
            end
            if found then
                targetPlayer = found
            else
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ 오류 ]", Text = "플레이어를 찾을 수 없습니다: " .. namePart, Duration = 3})
                return
            end
        end

        if not TNUM or TNUM <= 0 then return end

        local oldSession = L.lpPendikSessions[targetPlayer]
        if oldSession then
            oldSession.enabled = false
            if oldSession.charConn then
                oldSession.charConn:Disconnect()
                oldSession.charConn = nil
            end
        end

        local session = {
            enabled = true,
            count = TNUM,
            wasDead = false,
            charConn = nil,
            chainToys = {},
            notAttachedStreak = 0,
            standbyReady = false,
            standbyHoldConns = {},
            standbyWLast = nil,
            wasInPlot = false,
            standbyPreparing = false
        }
        L.lpPendikSessions[targetPlayer] = session

        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ LpPendik 시작 ]", Text = targetPlayer.DisplayName .. "님에게 연필 " .. TNUM .. "개를 연결해서 반복 부착 시작...", Duration = 3})

        local function lpPendikFindToyFolders()
            local folders = {}
            local myFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild(lp.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
            if myFolder then table.insert(folders, myFolder) end
            local plotItems = workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
            if plotItems then
                for _, plotFolder in ipairs(plotItems:GetChildren()) do
                    table.insert(folders, plotFolder)
                end
            end
            return folders
        end

        local function lpPendikIsChainAttached(attachTarget)
            for _, folder in ipairs(lpPendikFindToyFolders()) do
                for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                    if child.Name == "ToolPencil" then
                        local weld = child:FindFirstChild("StickyWeld", true)
                        if weld and (weld.Part1 == attachTarget or weld.Part0 == attachTarget) then
                            return true
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        local function lpPendikDestroyChain(chainToys)
            for _, toy in ipairs(chainToys) do
                if toy and toy.Parent then
                    pcall(function()
                        (game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("MenuToys"):WaitForChild("DestroyToy") :: any):FireServer(toy)
                    end)
                end
            end
        end

        local function lpPendikSpawnAndHold(beforeSet)
            local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            local SpawnToy = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("MenuToys"):WaitForChild("SpawnToyRemoteFunction")
            
            while not (lp and lp:FindFirstChild("CanSpawnToy") and lp.CanSpawnToy.Value) do
                if not L.lpPendikSessions[targetPlayer] or L.lpPendikSessions[targetPlayer] ~= session or not session.enabled then return nil end
                task.wait(0.1)
            end

            local myChar = lp.Character
            if not myChar then return nil end
            local myHRP = myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not myHRP then return nil end

            pcall(function()
                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                    SpawnToy:InvokeServer("ToolPencil", myHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 3, 8), Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
                end))
            end)
            task.wait(0.3)

            local pencil = nil
            for _, folder in ipairs(lpPendikFindToyFolders()) do
                for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                    if child.Name == "ToolPencil" and not beforeSet[child] then
                        pencil = child
                        break
                    end
                end
                if pencil then break end
            end

            if not pencil then return nil end

            for _, part in ipairs(pencil:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    pcall(function()
                        part.CanCollide = false
                        part.CanQuery = false
                        part.CanTouch = false
                    end)
                end
            end

            local stickyPart, SoundPart = pencil:FindFirstChild("StickyPart"), pencil:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")
            if not stickyPart or not SoundPart then return pencil, nil end

            pcall(function()
                ReplicatedStorage.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(stickyPart, stickyPart.CFrame)
                ReplicatedStorage.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(stickyPart, myHRP.CFrame)
                ReplicatedStorage.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(SoundPart, SoundPart.CFrame)
                ReplicatedStorage.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(SoundPart, myHRP.CFrame)
            end)
            task.wait(0.05)

            local holdCF = CFrame.new(0, 100000, 0)
            pcall(function() stickyPart.CFrame = holdCF end)
            pcall(function() SoundPart.CFrame = holdCF end)

            local holdConn
            holdConn = getgenv().__DST(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not stickyPart or not stickyPart.Parent then
                    if holdConn then holdConn:Disconnect() end
                    return
                end
                pcall(function() stickyPart.CFrame = holdCF end)
                pcall(function() SoundPart.CFrame = holdCF end)
            end))

            return pencil, holdConn
        end

        local function lpPendikPrepareStandbyChain()
            if session.standbyReady or session.standbyPreparing then return end
            session.standbyPreparing = true
            while L.lpPendikSpawnMutex do task.wait(0.05) end
            L.lpPendikSpawnMutex = true
            local function release() L.lpPendikSpawnMutex = false session.standbyPreparing = false end

            local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            local StickyPartEvent = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("PlayerEvents"):WaitForChild("StickyPartEvent")

            local pencils, holdConns = {}, {}

            for i = 1, session.count do
                if not L.lpPendikSessions[targetPlayer] or L.lpPendikSessions[targetPlayer] ~= session or not session.enabled then
                    for _, c in pairs(holdConns) do if c then c:Disconnect() end end
                    release() return
                end
                
                local beforeSet = {}
                for _, folder in ipairs(lpPendikFindToyFolders()) do
                    for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                        if child.Name == "ToolPencil" then beforeSet[child] = true end
                    end
                end

                local pencil, holdConn = lpPendikSpawnAndHold(beforeSet)
                while not pencil do
                    if not L.lpPendikSessions[targetPlayer] or L.lpPendikSessions[targetPlayer] ~= session or not session.enabled then
                        for _, c in pairs(holdConns) do if c then c:Disconnect() end end
                        release() return
                    end
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    pencil, holdConn = lpPendikSpawnAndHold(beforeSet)
                end
                if pencil then
                    local stickyPart = pencil:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
                    if stickyPart then
                        stickyPart.Name = "w" .. i
                        for _, part in ipairs(pencil:GetDescendants()) do
                            if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Color == Color3.fromRGB(158, 108, 141) then
                                part.Name = "a" .. i
                            end
                        end
                        table.insert(pencils, pencil)
                        holdConns[pencil] = holdConn
                        table.insert(session.chainToys, pencil)
                        L.lpPendikKnownToys[pencil] = true
                    elseif holdConn then
                        holdConn:Disconnect()
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.1)
            end

            for i = 1, #pencils - 1 do
                local currentPencil, nextPencil = pencils[i], pencils[i + 1]
                if currentPencil and nextPencil then
                    local attachFrom, attachTo = currentPencil:FindFirstChild("w" .. i), nextPencil:FindFirstChild("a" .. (i + 1))
                    if attachFrom and attachTo then
                        if holdConns[currentPencil] then holdConns[currentPencil]:Disconnect() holdConns[currentPencil] = nil end
                        pcall(function() StickyPartEvent:FireServer(attachFrom, attachTo, CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(-90), 0)) end)
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.05)
            end

            local lastPencil = pencils[session.count]
            local wLast = lastPencil and lastPencil:FindFirstChild("w" .. session.count)
            
            session.standbyHoldConns = holdConns
            session.standbyWLast = wLast
            session.standbyReady = true
            release()
        end

        local function lpPendikAttachStandby(attachTarget)
            if not session.standbyReady or not session.standbyWLast then return false end
            local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            local StickyPartEvent = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("PlayerEvents"):WaitForChild("StickyPartEvent")
            
            for _, c in pairs(session.standbyHoldConns) do
                if c then c:Disconnect() end
            end
            session.standbyHoldConns = {}
            
            pcall(function()
                StickyPartEvent:FireServer(session.standbyWLast, attachTarget, CFrame.new(0, -1.1, 0.1) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), 0))
            end)
            
            session.standbyReady = false
            session.standbyWLast = nil
            session.lastAttachTime = os.clock() 
            return true
        end

        local function lpPendikEnsureAttached()
            local s = L.lpPendikSessions[targetPlayer]
            if not s or s ~= session or not s.enabled then return end

            local tChar = targetPlayer.Character
            local tHum = tChar and tChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            
            if not tChar or not tHum or tHum.Health <= 0 then
                if not session.wasDead then
                    session.wasDead = true
                    if #session.chainToys > 0 then
                        lpPendikDestroyChain(session.chainToys)
                        for _, toy in ipairs(session.chainToys) do L.lpPendikKnownToys[toy] = nil end
                        session.chainToys = {}
                    end
                    if targetPlayer ~= lp and not session.standbyReady then
                        getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(lpPendikPrepareStandbyChain))
                    end
                end
                return
            end

            local tHRP = tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local attachTarget = tHRP and tHRP:FindFirstChild("RagdollTouchedHitbox")
            if not attachTarget then return end

            local myChar = lp.Character
            local myHum = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not myChar or not myHum or myHum.Health <= 0 then return end

            local targetInPlot = isTargetInPlot(targetPlayer)
            if targetInPlot then
                session.wasInPlot = true

                local plotAliveCount = 0
                for i, toy in ipairs(session.chainToys) do
                    if toy and toy.Parent and toy:FindFirstChild("w" .. i) then
                        plotAliveCount = plotAliveCount + 1
                    end
                end

                if plotAliveCount < session.count and not session.standbyPreparing then
                    session.standbyReady = false
                    session.standbyWLast = nil
                    for _, c in pairs(session.standbyHoldConns) do
                        if c then c:Disconnect() end
                    end
                    session.standbyHoldConns = {}

                    if #session.chainToys > 0 then
                        lpPendikDestroyChain(session.chainToys)
                        for _, toy in ipairs(session.chainToys) do L.lpPendikKnownToys[toy] = nil end
                        session.chainToys = {}
                    end

                    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(lpPendikPrepareStandbyChain))
                end
                return
            elseif session.wasInPlot then
                session.wasInPlot = false
                if not session.standbyReady then
                    lpPendikPrepareStandbyChain()
                end
                if lpPendikAttachStandby(attachTarget) then
                    session.lastAttachTime = os.clock()
                    return
                end
            end

            session.lastAttachTime = session.lastAttachTime or 0
            if os.clock() - session.lastAttachTime < 1.5 then
                return
            end

            local isAttached = lpPendikIsChainAttached(attachTarget)
            
            local aliveCount = 0
            for i, toy in ipairs(session.chainToys) do
                if toy and toy.Parent and toy:FindFirstChild("w" .. i) then
                    aliveCount = aliveCount + 1
                end
            end

            if not isAttached or aliveCount < session.count then
                session.notAttachedStreak = (session.notAttachedStreak or 0) + 1
                
                if aliveCount < session.count or session.notAttachedStreak >= 3 then
                    session.notAttachedStreak = 0
                    
                    if #session.chainToys > 0 then
                        lpPendikDestroyChain(session.chainToys)
                        for _, toy in ipairs(session.chainToys) do L.lpPendikKnownToys[toy] = nil end
                        session.chainToys = {}
                    end

                    if targetPlayer == lp then
                        session.standbyReady = false
                        lpPendikPrepareStandbyChain()
                        lpPendikAttachStandby(attachTarget)
                    else
                        if not session.standbyReady then
                            lpPendikPrepareStandbyChain()
                        end
                        lpPendikAttachStandby(attachTarget)
                    end
                    
                    session.lastAttachTime = os.clock()
                end
            else
                session.notAttachedStreak = 0
            end
        end

        session.charConn = getgenv().__DST(targetPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
            local s = L.lpPendikSessions[targetPlayer]
            if not s or s ~= session or not s.enabled then return end
            
            getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                local newHrp = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
                local attachTarget = newHrp and newHrp:WaitForChild("RagdollTouchedHitbox", 5)
                
                if attachTarget then
                    if targetPlayer ~= lp then
                        local startTime = os.clock()
                        while os.clock() - startTime < 1.0 do
                            if session.standbyReady then break end
                            task.wait(0.02)
                        end
                        if session.standbyReady then
                            lpPendikAttachStandby(attachTarget)
                        end
                    end
                end
                
                task.wait(0.05)
                session.wasDead = false
            end))
        end))

        getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
            while true do
                local s = L.lpPendikSessions[targetPlayer]
                if not s or s ~= session or not s.enabled then break end
                lpPendikEnsureAttached()
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
        end))

    elseif lowerMsg == ".unlppendik" then
        for player, s in pairs(L.lpPendikSessions) do
            s.enabled = false
            if s.charConn then
                s.charConn:Disconnect()
                s.charConn = nil
            end
        end
        L.lpPendikSessions = {}
        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ LpPendik 종료 ]", Text = "연필 반복 부착을 전체 중지했습니다.", Duration = 3})

    elseif lowerMsg:sub(1, 6) == ".bang " or lowerMsg == ".bang" then
        stopBang()

        local args = {}
        for w in string.gmatch(msg:sub(7), "%S+") do
            table.insert(args, w)
        end

        local char = lp.Character
        local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not humanoid then
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ 오류 ]", Text = "캐릭터를 찾을 수 없습니다.", Duration = 3})
            return
        end

        M.bangAnimation = getgenv().__DSN("Animation")
        M.bangAnimation.AnimationId = humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15
            and "rbxassetid://5918726674"
            or "rbxassetid://148840371"

        local nameArg, speedArg = args[1], tonumber(args[2])
        if args[1] and tonumber(args[1]) and not args[2] then
            nameArg, speedArg = nil, tonumber(args[1])
        end

        M.bangAnimTrack = humanoid:LoadAnimation(M.bangAnimation)
        M.bangAnimTrack:Play(0.1, 1, 1)
        M.bangAnimTrack:AdjustSpeed(speedArg or 3)

        M.bangDiedConnection = getgenv().__DST(humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
            if M.bangAnimTrack then
                M.bangAnimTrack:Stop()
            end
            if M.bangAnimation then
                M.bangAnimation:Destroy()
                M.bangAnimation = nil
            end
            if M.bangDiedConnection then
                M.bangDiedConnection:Disconnect()
                M.bangDiedConnection = nil
            end
            if M.bangLoopConnection then
                M.bangLoopConnection:Disconnect()
                M.bangLoopConnection = nil
            end
            bangFakeTP(nil, false)
        end))

        if nameArg then
            local namePart, targetPlayer = nameArg:lower(), nil
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p ~= lp then
                    local pName, pDisplay = p.Name:lower(), p.DisplayName:lower()
                    if pName:sub(1, #namePart) == namePart or pDisplay:sub(1, #namePart) == namePart then
                        targetPlayer = p
                        break
                    end
                end
            end

            if targetPlayer then
                M.bangTarget = targetPlayer
                M.bangOffset = CFrame.new(0, 0, 1.1)
                bangFakeTP(targetPlayer, true, M.bangOffset)

                local function hookBangTargetHumanoid(character)
                    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                        local h = character and (character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5))
                        if not h then return end
                        if M.bangTargetDiedConn then M.bangTargetDiedConn:Disconnect() end
                        M.bangTargetDiedConn = getgenv().__DST(h.Died:Connect(function()
                            bangFakeTP(nil, false)
                        end))
                    end))
                end
                hookBangTargetHumanoid(targetPlayer.Character)
                if M.bangTargetRespawnConn then M.bangTargetRespawnConn:Disconnect() end
                M.bangTargetRespawnConn = getgenv().__DST(targetPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
                    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                        newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
                        if M.bangTarget == targetPlayer and M.bangOffset then
                            bangFakeTP(targetPlayer, true, M.bangOffset)
                        end
                        hookBangTargetHumanoid(newChar)
                    end))
                end))
                if M.bangTargetLeaveConn then M.bangTargetLeaveConn:Disconnect() end
                M.bangTargetLeaveConn = getgenv().__DST(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
                    if player == targetPlayer then
                        stopBang()
                        notifyTargetLeaveStop("Bang", targetPlayer)
                    end
                end))
            else
