                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ 오류 ]", Text = "플레이어를 찾을 수 없습니다: " .. nameArg, Duration = 3})
            end
        end

    elseif lowerMsg == ".unbang" then
        stopBang()
        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ Bang 종료 ]", Text = "Bang을 중지했습니다.", Duration = 3})

    elseif lowerMsg == ".vtp" or lowerMsg == ".voidtp" then
        if M.voidTpEnabled then
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ Void TP ]", Text = "이미 켜져 있습니다.", Duration = 2})
        else
            M.voidTpEnabled = true
            local char = lp.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                M.voidTpOriginalPosition = hrp.CFrame
            end
            AntiGrabTP_ActiveV1 = true
            if M.voidTpHBC then M.voidTpHBC:Disconnect() end
            M.voidTpHBC = getgenv().__DST(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not M.voidTpEnabled or not AntiGrabTP_ActiveV1 then return end
                if _killInProgress then return end
                local c = lp.Character
                local hrpNow = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not hrpNow then return end
                local randomX, randomZ = math.random(-9999999, 9999999), math.random(-9999999, 9999999)
                hrpNow.CFrame = CFrame.new(randomX, 9999999, randomZ)
            end))
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ Void TP 켜짐 ]", Text = "Void TP가 작동합니다.", Duration = 3})
        end
    elseif lowerMsg == ".unvtp" or lowerMsg == ".unvoidtp" then
        if not M.voidTpEnabled then
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ Void TP ]", Text = "이미 꺼져 있습니다.", Duration = 2})
        else
            M.voidTpEnabled = false
            AntiGrabTP_ActiveV1 = false
            if M.voidTpHBC then
                M.voidTpHBC:Disconnect()
                M.voidTpHBC = nil
            end
            local char = lp.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp and M.voidTpOriginalPosition then
                hrp.CFrame = M.voidTpOriginalPosition
            end
            M.voidTpOriginalPosition = nil
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ Void TP 꺼짐 ]", Text = "원래 자리로 복귀했습니다.", Duration = 3})
        end
    elseif lowerMsg == ".ltp" or lowerMsg == ".looptp" then
        if M.loopTpEnabled then
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ Loop TP ]", Text = "이미 켜져 있습니다.", Duration = 2})
        else
            M.loopTpEnabled = true
            local char = lp.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                M.loopTpOriginalPosition = hrp.CFrame
            end
            AntiGrabTP_ActiveV2 = true
            if M.loopTpHBC then M.loopTpHBC:Disconnect() end
            M.loopTpHBC = getgenv().__DST(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not M.loopTpEnabled or not AntiGrabTP_ActiveV2 then return end
                if _killInProgress then return end
                local c = lp.Character
                local hrpNow = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not hrpNow then return end
                local randomX, randomY, randomZ = math.random(-1500, 1500), math.random(0, 1500), math.random(-1500, 1500)
                hrpNow.CFrame = CFrame.new(randomX, randomY, randomZ)
            end))
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ Loop TP 켜짐 ]", Text = "Loop TP가 작동합니다.", Duration = 3})
        end
    elseif lowerMsg == ".unltp" or lowerMsg == ".unlooptp" then
        if not M.loopTpEnabled then
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ Loop TP ]", Text = "이미 꺼져 있습니다.", Duration = 2})
        else
            M.loopTpEnabled = false
            AntiGrabTP_ActiveV2 = false
            if M.loopTpHBC then
                M.loopTpHBC:Disconnect()
                M.loopTpHBC = nil
            end
            local char = lp.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp and M.loopTpOriginalPosition then
                hrp.CFrame = M.loopTpOriginalPosition
            end
            M.loopTpOriginalPosition = nil
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ Void TP/Loop TP 꺼짐 ]", Text = "원래 자리로 복귀했습니다.", Duration = 3})
        end

    elseif lowerMsg:sub(1, 6) == ".lpwd " or lowerMsg:sub(1, 8) == ".loopwd " then
        local argStart = lowerMsg:sub(1, 6) == ".lpwd " and 7 or 9
        local rawArg, nameList = lowerMsg:sub(argStart):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$"), {}
        for part in rawArg:gmatch("[^,]+") do
            local trimmed = part:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
            if trimmed ~= "" then
                table.insert(nameList, trimmed)
            end
        end

        local foundPlayers, notFound = {}, {}
        for _, namePart in ipairs(nameList) do
            local matched = nil
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p ~= lp then
                    local pName, pDisplay = p.Name:lower(), p.DisplayName:lower()
                    if pName:sub(1, #namePart) == namePart or pDisplay:sub(1, #namePart) == namePart then
                        local dup = false
                        for _, fp in ipairs(foundPlayers) do
                            if fp == p then dup = true; break end
                        end
                        if not dup then matched = p end
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            if matched then
                table.insert(foundPlayers, matched)
            else
                table.insert(notFound, namePart)
            end
        end

        if #foundPlayers == 0 then
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "[ LpWD ]",
                Text = "일치하는 플레이어를 찾을 수 없습니다: " .. rawArg,
                Duration = 3
            })
        else
            if #notFound > 0 then
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = "[ LpWD 일부 미발견 ]",
                    Text = table.concat(notFound, ", ") .. " — 찾지 못함. " .. #foundPlayers .. "명으로 진행합니다.",
                    Duration = 4
                })
                task.wait(0.3)
            end

            L.lpwdEnabled = false
            if L.lpwdLoop then task.cancel(L.lpwdLoop); L.lpwdLoop = nil end
            for _, conn in pairs(L.lpwdLeaveConns) do pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
            L.lpwdLeaveConns = {}

            L.lpwdTargets = foundPlayers
            L.lpwdEnabled = true

            local function syncToFtap(playerList)
                local listRef = nil
                if type(getgenv().playersInLoop1V) == "table" then
                    listRef = getgenv().playersInLoop1V
                elseif type((getfenv() :: any).playersInLoop1V) == "table" then
                    listRef = (getfenv() :: any).playersInLoop1V
                end
                if not listRef then return end
                for _, p in ipairs(playerList) do
                    local already = false
                    for _, v in ipairs(listRef) do
                        if v == p.Name then already = true; break end
                    end
                    if not already then
                        table.insert(listRef, p.Name)
                    end
                end
            end
            syncToFtap(L.lpwdTargets)

            if type(getgenv().AntiStickyGBF) == "function" then
                getgenv().AntiStickyGBT = true
                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(getgenv().AntiStickyGBF))
            elseif type((getfenv() :: any).AntiStickyGBF) == "function" then
                (getfenv() :: any).AntiStickyGBT = true
                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn((getfenv() :: any).AntiStickyGBF))
            else
                L.lpwdLoop = getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                    local rs_wd = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

                    local function getInv()
                        return workspace:FindFirstChild(lp.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                    end

                    while L.lpwdEnabled do
                      repeat
                        local char = lp.Character
                        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if not hrp then task.wait(0.1); break end

                        local inv_wd = getInv()
                        if not inv_wd then task.wait(0.1); break end

                        local desk = inv_wd:FindFirstChild("WD")
                        if not desk then
                            local spawnRemote = rs_wd:FindFirstChild("MenuToys") and rs_wd.MenuToys:FindFirstChild("SpawnToyRemoteFunction")
                            if spawnRemote then
                                local offset = hrp.CFrame.LookVector * -24
                                local spawnCF = hrp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) + offset
                                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                                    pcall(function()
                                        spawnRemote:InvokeServer("SprayCanWD", spawnCF, spawnCF.Position)
                                    end)
                                end))
                                task.wait(0.05)
                                for _, item in pairs(inv_wd:GetChildren()) do
                                    if item.Name == "SprayCanWD" then
                                        item.Name = "WD"
                                        desk = item
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            if not desk then task.wait(0.1); break end
                        end

                        local main = desk:FindFirstChild("Main")
                        if main then
                            local bp = main:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyPosition")
                            if not bp then
                                bp = getgenv().__DSN("BodyPosition")
                                bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                                bp.Position = Vector3.new(hrp.CFrame.X, 600, hrp.CFrame.Z)
                                bp.Parent = main
                            else
                                bp.Position = Vector3.new(hrp.CFrame.X, 600, hrp.CFrame.Z)
                            end
                        end

                        local hitbox = desk:FindFirstChild("Hitbox")
                        if not hitbox then task.wait(0.1); break end

                        local partOwner = hitbox:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                        if not partOwner or partOwner.Value ~= lp.Name then
                            rs_wd.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(hitbox, hitbox.CFrame)
                            task.wait(0.1)
                            partOwner = hitbox:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                            if not partOwner or partOwner.Value ~= lp.Name then
                                task.wait(0.1); break
                            end
                        end

                        local hitbox2 = desk:FindFirstChild("StickyRemoverPart")
                        if not hitbox2 then task.wait(0.1); break end

                        local plotItemsFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
                        local playersInPlotsF, validTargets =
                            plotItemsFolder and plotItemsFolder:FindFirstChild("PlayersInPlots"),
                            {}
                        for _, tp in ipairs(L.lpwdTargets) do
                            if tp and tp.Character then
                                local tRoot, inPlot =
                                    tp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"),
                                    playersInPlotsF and playersInPlotsF:FindFirstChild(tp.Name)
                                if tRoot and not inPlot then
                                    table.insert(validTargets, tRoot)
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        for _, tRoot in ipairs(validTargets) do
                            hitbox2.CFrame = tRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(1, 0, 3)
                            task.wait()
                        end

                        task.wait(0.05)
                      until true
                    end
                end))
            end

            for _, tp in ipairs(L.lpwdTargets) do
                local conn
                conn = getgenv().__DST(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
                    if p == tp then
                        for i, v in ipairs(L.lpwdTargets) do
                            if v == p then table.remove(L.lpwdTargets, i); break end
                        end
                        local listRef = type(getgenv().playersInLoop1V) == "table" and getgenv().playersInLoop1V
                            or (type((getfenv() :: any).playersInLoop1V) == "table" and (getfenv() :: any).playersInLoop1V or nil)
                        if listRef then
                            for i, v in ipairs(listRef) do
                                if v == p.Name then table.remove(listRef, i); break end
                            end
                        end
                        if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
                        L.lpwdLeaveConns[p.UserId] = nil

                        if #L.lpwdTargets == 0 then
                            L.lpwdEnabled = false
                            if L.lpwdLoop then task.cancel(L.lpwdLoop); L.lpwdLoop = nil end
                            pcall(function() if type(getgenv().AntiStickyGBT) ~= "nil" then getgenv().AntiStickyGBT = false end end)
                            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                Title = "[ Loop WD 자동 중지 ]",
                                Text = "모든 타겟이 서버를 나가 Loop WD가 중지되었습니다.",
                                Duration = 4
                            })
                        else
                            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                Title = "[ Loop WD 타겟 제거 ]",
                                Text = p.Name .. "님이 나가 타겟에서 제거됐습니다. 남은 타겟: " .. #L.lpwdTargets .. "명",
                                Duration = 3
                            })
                        end
                    end
                end))
                L.lpwdLeaveConns[tp.UserId] = conn
            end

            local savedNames = {}
            for _, p in ipairs(L.lpwdTargets) do table.insert(savedNames, p.Name) end

            local nameStr = table.concat(savedNames, ", ")
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "[ Loop WD 켜짐 | " .. #L.lpwdTargets .. "명 ]",
                Text = nameStr .. " — WD 루프를 시작합니다.",
                Duration = 4
            })
        end

    elseif lowerMsg == ".unlpwd" or lowerMsg == ".unloopwd" then
        if not L.lpwdEnabled then
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "[ Loop WD ]",
                Text = "Loop WD가 실행 중이 아닙니다.",
                Duration = 2
            })
        else
            L.lpwdEnabled = false
            if L.lpwdLoop then task.cancel(L.lpwdLoop); L.lpwdLoop = nil end

            for _, conn in pairs(L.lpwdLeaveConns) do pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
            L.lpwdLeaveConns = {}

            pcall(function() if type(getgenv().AntiStickyGBT) ~= "nil" then getgenv().AntiStickyGBT = false end end)
            pcall(function() (getfenv() :: any).AntiStickyGBT = false end)

            local listRef = type(getgenv().playersInLoop1V) == "table" and getgenv().playersInLoop1V
                or (type((getfenv() :: any).playersInLoop1V) == "table" and (getfenv() :: any).playersInLoop1V or nil)
            if listRef then
                for _, tp in ipairs(L.lpwdTargets) do
                    for i, v in ipairs(listRef) do
                        if v == tp.Name then table.remove(listRef, i); break end
                    end
                end
            end

            local removedNames = {}
            for _, p in ipairs(L.lpwdTargets) do table.insert(removedNames, p.Name) end
            L.lpwdTargets = {}

            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "[ Loop WD 꺼짐 ]",
                Text = (#removedNames > 0 and table.concat(removedNames, ", ") .. " — " or "") .. "Loop WD가 중지되었습니다.",
                Duration = 3
            })
        end

    elseif lowerMsg == ".bl" or lowerMsg == ".blacklist" then
        toggleBlackListGui()

    elseif lowerMsg:sub(1, 4) == ".bl " or lowerMsg:sub(1, 11) == ".blacklist " then
        local argStr
        if lowerMsg:sub(1, 4) == ".bl " then
            argStr = msg:sub(5):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        else
            argStr = msg:sub(12):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        end
        if argStr == "" then
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ BlackList ]", Text = "사용법: .bl [이름 또는 @아이디]", Duration = 3})
        else
            local cleanArg, isOffline = argStr:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$"), false
            if cleanArg:sub(1, 1) == "@" then
                isOffline = true
                cleanArg = cleanArg:sub(2)
            end
            cleanArg = cleanArg:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
            local cleanArgLower = cleanArg:lower()

            if cleanArg == "" then
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ BlackList ]", Text = "사용법: .bl [이름 또는 @아이디]", Duration = 3})
            elseif isOffline then
                local key = cleanArgLower
                if blackList[key] then
                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ BlackList ]", Text = cleanArg .. "님은 이미 블랙리스트에 있습니다.", Duration = 3})
                else
                    blackList[key] = cleanArg
                    saveBlackList()
                    if F.blackListGui and F.blackListGui.Enabled then
                        updateBlackListGui()
                    end
                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ BlackList 추가 ]", Text = "[오프라인] " .. cleanArg .. "님을 블랙리스트에 추가했습니다.\n향후 입장 시 자동 감지됩니다.", Duration = 5})
                end
            else
                local matched, kw = nil, cleanArgLower
                for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= lp then
                        if p.Name:lower():sub(1, #kw) == kw or p.DisplayName:lower():sub(1, #kw) == kw then
                            matched = p
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
                if matched then
                    local key = matched.Name:lower()
                    if blackList[key] then
                        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ BlackList ]", Text = matched.Name .. "님은 이미 블랙리스트에 있습니다.", Duration = 3})
                    else
                        blackList[key] = matched.Name
                        saveBlackList()
                        if F.blackListGui and F.blackListGui.Enabled then
                            updateBlackListGui()
                        end
                        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ BlackList 추가 ]", Text = matched.DisplayName .. " (@" .. matched.Name .. ")님을 블랙리스트에 추가했습니다.", Duration = 4})
                    end
                else
                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ BlackList ]", Text = "'" .. cleanArg .. "' 와 매칭되는 플레이어를 찾지 못했습니다.", Duration = 3})
                end
            end
        end

    elseif lowerMsg == ".unbl" or lowerMsg:sub(1, 6) == ".unbl " or lowerMsg == ".unblacklist" or lowerMsg:sub(1, 13) == ".unblacklist " then
        local argStr
        if lowerMsg:sub(1, 6) == ".unbl " then
            argStr = msg:sub(7):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        elseif lowerMsg:sub(1, 13) == ".unblacklist " then
            argStr = msg:sub(14):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        else
            argStr = ""
        end
        if argStr == "" then
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ BlackList ]", Text = "사용법: .unbl [이름 또는 @아이디]", Duration = 3})
        else
            local cleanArg = argStr
            if cleanArg:sub(1, 1) == "@" then
                cleanArg = cleanArg:sub(2)
            end
            cleanArg = cleanArg:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
            local key = cleanArg:lower()
            
            if blackList[key] then
                local removed = blackList[key]
                blackList[key] = nil
                saveBlackList()
                if F.blackListGui and F.blackListGui.Enabled then
                    updateBlackListGui()
                end
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ BlackList 제거 ]", Text = removed .. "님을 블랙리스트에서 제거했습니다.", Duration = 4})
            else
                local matches = {}
                for storedKey, storedValue in pairs(blackList) do
                    local storedLower = storedValue:lower()
                    if storedLower:sub(1, #key) == key then
                        table.insert(matches, storedKey)
                    else
                        local player = Players:FindFirstChild(storedValue)
                        if player and player.DisplayName:lower():sub(1, #key) == key then
                            table.insert(matches, storedKey)
                        end
                    end
                end

                if #matches == 1 then
                    local found = matches[1]
                    local removed = blackList[found]
                    blackList[found] = nil
                    saveBlackList()
                    if F.blackListGui and F.blackListGui.Enabled then
                        updateBlackListGui()
                    end
                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ BlackList 제거 ]", Text = removed .. "님을 블랙리스트에서 제거했습니다.", Duration = 4})
                elseif #matches > 1 then
                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ BlackList ]", Text = "중복 이름입니다. 더 길게 입력해주세요.", Duration = 4})
                else
                    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ BlackList ]", Text = cleanArg .. "님은 블랙리스트에 없습니다.", Duration = 3})
                end
            end
        end

    elseif lowerMsg == ".bllist" then
        toggleBlackListGui()

    elseif lowerMsg:sub(1, 7) == ".orbit " or lowerMsg == ".orbit" then
        stopOrbit()

        local args = {}
        for w in string.gmatch(msg:sub(7), "%S+") do
            table.insert(args, w)
        end

        local namePart, speedArg, distArg = args[1], args[2], args[3]
        local targetPlayer = nil
        if namePart then
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p.Name:lower():sub(1, #namePart) == namePart:lower() or p.DisplayName:lower():sub(1, #namePart) == namePart:lower() then
                    targetPlayer = p
                    break
                end
            end
        end

        if targetPlayer then
            M.orbitEnabled = true
            M.orbitTarget = targetPlayer
            M.orbitSpeed = tonumber(speedArg) or 0.2
            M.orbitDistance = tonumber(distArg) or 6

            orbitFakeTP(targetPlayer, true)

            local function hookOrbitTargetHumanoid(character)
                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                    local h = character and (character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5))
                    if not h then return end
                    if M.orbitTargetDiedConn then M.orbitTargetDiedConn:Disconnect() end
                    M.orbitTargetDiedConn = getgenv().__DST(h.Died:Connect(function()
                        orbitFakeTP(nil, false)
                    end))
                end))
            end
            hookOrbitTargetHumanoid(targetPlayer.Character)
            M.orbitTargetRespawnConn = getgenv().__DST(targetPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
                if M.orbitEnabled and M.orbitTarget == targetPlayer then
                    orbitFakeTP(targetPlayer, true)
                end
                hookOrbitTargetHumanoid(newChar)
            end))
            if M.orbitTargetLeaveConn then M.orbitTargetLeaveConn:Disconnect() end
            M.orbitTargetLeaveConn = getgenv().__DST(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
                if player == targetPlayer then
                    stopOrbit()
                    notifyTargetLeaveStop("Orbit", targetPlayer)
                end
            end))

            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ Orbit ]", Text = "끊임없는 텔레포트 궤도 돌기를 시작했습니다: " .. (targetPlayer.DisplayName or targetPlayer.Name), Duration = 3})
        else
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ 오류 ]", Text = "대상 플레이어를 찾을 수 없습니다. 예: .orbit 이름 [속도] [거리]", Duration = 3})
        end

    elseif lowerMsg == ".unorbit" then
        stopOrbit()
        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ Orbit ]", Text = "궤도 돌기를 중지했습니다.", Duration = 3})

    elseif lowerMsg:sub(1, 9) == ".headsit " or lowerMsg == ".headsit" then
        if M.headSitConnection then M.headSitConnection:Disconnect() M.headSitConnection = nil end
        if M.headSitTargetLeaveConn then M.headSitTargetLeaveConn:Disconnect() M.headSitTargetLeaveConn = nil end
        if M.headSitRespawnConn then M.headSitRespawnConn:Disconnect() M.headSitRespawnConn = nil end
        if M.headSitDiedConn then M.headSitDiedConn:Disconnect() M.headSitDiedConn = nil end
        M.headSitTarget = nil

        local namePart = msg:sub(10):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        local targetPlayer = nil
        if namePart and namePart ~= "" then
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p.Name:lower():sub(1, #namePart) == namePart:lower() or p.DisplayName:lower():sub(1, #namePart) == namePart:lower() then
                    targetPlayer = p
                    break
                end
            end
        end

        if targetPlayer then
            M.headSitTarget = targetPlayer

            local function headSitTrySit()
                local myChar = lp.Character
                local myHum = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if myHum then myHum.Sit = true end
            end

            local function headSitReleaseSit()
                local myChar = lp.Character
                local myHum = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if myHum then myHum.Sit = false end
            end

            local function hookHeadSitTargetHumanoid(character)
                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                    local h = character and (character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5))
                    if not h then return end
                    if M.headSitDiedConn then M.headSitDiedConn:Disconnect() end
                    M.headSitDiedConn = getgenv().__DST(h.Died:Connect(function()
                        if M.headSitTarget == targetPlayer then
                            headSitReleaseSit()
                        end
                    end))
                end))
            end

            headSitTrySit()
            hookHeadSitTargetHumanoid(targetPlayer.Character)

            local predict = M.newLeadPredictor()
            M.headSitConnection = getgenv().__DST(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if M.headSitTarget ~= targetPlayer then return end
                local myChar2 = lp.Character
                local myHum2 = myChar2 and myChar2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local myRoot2 = myChar2 and myChar2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not (myHum2 and myRoot2) then return end

                local tChar = targetPlayer.Character
                local tRoot = tChar and tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local tHum = tChar and tChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                if tChar and tRoot and tHum and tHum.Health > 0 then
                    if not myHum2.Sit then myHum2.Sit = true end
                    myRoot2.CFrame = tRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1.6, 0.4) + predict(tRoot, myRoot2)
                    myRoot2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
                -- 타겟이 죽어서 sit이 풀린 상태면 리스폰 전까지 강제로 다시 앉히지 않고 대기
            end))

            if M.headSitRespawnConn then M.headSitRespawnConn:Disconnect() end
            M.headSitRespawnConn = getgenv().__DST(targetPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
                if M.headSitTarget ~= targetPlayer then return end
                hookHeadSitTargetHumanoid(newChar)
                task.wait(0.3)
                if M.headSitTarget == targetPlayer then
                    headSitTrySit()
                end
            end))

            if M.headSitTargetLeaveConn then M.headSitTargetLeaveConn:Disconnect() end
            M.headSitTargetLeaveConn = getgenv().__DST(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
                if player == targetPlayer then
                    if M.headSitConnection then M.headSitConnection:Disconnect() M.headSitConnection = nil end
                    if M.headSitRespawnConn then M.headSitRespawnConn:Disconnect() M.headSitRespawnConn = nil end
                    if M.headSitDiedConn then M.headSitDiedConn:Disconnect() M.headSitDiedConn = nil end
                    if M.headSitTargetLeaveConn then M.headSitTargetLeaveConn:Disconnect() M.headSitTargetLeaveConn = nil end
                    M.headSitTarget = nil
                    headSitReleaseSit()
                    notifyTargetLeaveStop("HeadSit", targetPlayer)
                end
            end))

            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ HeadSit ]", Text = "대상 머리 위에 앉기를 시작했습니다: " .. (targetPlayer.DisplayName or targetPlayer.Name), Duration = 3})
        else
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ 오류 ]", Text = "대상 플레이어를 찾을 수 없습니다. 예: .headsit 이름", Duration = 3})
        end

    elseif lowerMsg == ".unheadsit" then
        if M.headSitConnection then M.headSitConnection:Disconnect() M.headSitConnection = nil end
        if M.headSitTargetLeaveConn then M.headSitTargetLeaveConn:Disconnect() M.headSitTargetLeaveConn = nil end
        if M.headSitRespawnConn then M.headSitRespawnConn:Disconnect() M.headSitRespawnConn = nil end
        if M.headSitDiedConn then M.headSitDiedConn:Disconnect() M.headSitDiedConn = nil end
        M.headSitTarget = nil
        do
            local myChar = lp.Character
            local myHum = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if myHum then myHum.Sit = false end
        end
        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ HeadSit ]", Text = "머리 위 앉기를 중지했습니다.", Duration = 3})

    elseif lowerMsg == ".sit" then
        local myChar = lp.Character
        local myHum = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if myHum then
            myHum.Sit = true
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ Sit ]", Text = "캐릭터를 앉혔습니다.", Duration = 2})
        else
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ 오류 ]", Text = "캐릭터를 찾을 수 없습니다.", Duration = 3})
        end

    elseif lowerMsg == ".종료" or lowerMsg == ".end" then
        _scriptTerminated = true
        getgenv().DShubScript = false
        pcall(Z.ShutdownCommands)
        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", { Title = "[ 스크립트 종료 ]", Text = "열려있는 모든 GUI를 닫고 스크립트를 완벽하게 종료합니다.", Duration = 4 })

        if chatConnection then chatConnection:Disconnect() chatConnection = nil end
        if mainInputConnection then mainInputConnection:Disconnect() mainInputConnection = nil end

        toggleAntiLag(false)
        toggleFpsBoost(false)
        A.antiAfkEnabled = false
        if A.antiAfkConnection then A.antiAfkConnection:Disconnect(); A.antiAfkConnection = nil end
        if A.antiAfkLoop then task.cancel(A.antiAfkLoop); A.antiAfkLoop = nil end
        stopFlying()
        toggleNoclip(false)
        M.walkflingEnabled = false
        toggleSpin(false)
        if getgenv then
            getgenv().PcldOwnerRunning = false
            if getgenv().ForceStopPcldLoop then
                pcall(function() getgenv().ForceStopPcldLoop:Disconnect() end)
                getgenv().ForceStopPcldLoop = nil
            end
            for _, pcld in pairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
                if pcld:FindFirstChild("PcldOutline") or pcld:FindFirstChild("PcldNameTag") or pcld.Name:match("%[.*%] %(.*%)") then
                    pcld.Name = "PlayerCharacterLocationDetector"
                    local outline = pcld:FindFirstChild("PcldOutline")
                    if outline then pcall(function() outline:Destroy() end) end
                    local tag = pcld:FindFirstChild("PcldNameTag")
                    if tag then pcall(function() tag:Destroy() end) end
                    for _, child in pairs(pcld:GetChildren()) do
                        if child:IsA("BoolValue") then pcall(function() child:Destroy() end) end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        toggleAutoReset(false)
        stopLoopKill()
        toggleNameEsp(false)

        A.charAntiLagEnabled = false

        if A.autoAntiLagLoop then task.cancel(A.autoAntiLagLoop); A.autoAntiLagLoop = nil end

        if M.voidTpEnabled then
            M.voidTpEnabled = false
            AntiGrabTP_ActiveV1 = false
        end
        if M.loopTpEnabled then
            M.loopTpEnabled = false
            AntiGrabTP_ActiveV2 = false
        end

        if L.lpwdEnabled then
            L.lpwdEnabled = false
            if L.lpwdLoop then task.cancel(L.lpwdLoop); L.lpwdLoop = nil end
            for _, conn in pairs(L.lpwdLeaveConns) do pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
            L.lpwdLeaveConns = {}
            pcall(function() if type(getgenv().AntiStickyGBT) ~= "nil" then getgenv().AntiStickyGBT = false end end)
            L.lpwdTargets = {}
        end

        if getgenv().AntiBombToggle then
            getgenv().AntiBombToggle = false
            if getgenv().AntiBombOven then
                pcall(function() getgenv().AntiBombOven:Destroy() end)
                getgenv().AntiBombOven = nil
            end
        end

        toggleAntiInPlots(false)
        toggleNoclipBarrier(false)
        toggleOceanWalk(false)
        stopTpwalk()
        toggleIconEsp(false)
        toggleStickyPartEsp(false)
        stopLoopGoto()
        stopLpBring()
        toggleloopreset(false)

        if S.spawnConnection then S.spawnConnection:Disconnect(); S.spawnConnection = nil end
        if S.fullbrightLoop then S.fullbrightLoop:Disconnect(); S.fullbrightLoop = nil end
        if A.antiAfkConnection then A.antiAfkConnection:Disconnect(); A.antiAfkConnection = nil end
        if A.antiAfkLoop then task.cancel(A.antiAfkLoop); A.antiAfkLoop = nil end; A.antiAfkEnabled = false
        if A.antiLagLoop then task.cancel(A.antiLagLoop); A.antiLagLoop = nil end; A.antiLagEnabled = false
        if S.fpsBoostConnection then S.fpsBoostConnection:Disconnect(); S.fpsBoostConnection = nil end; S.fpsBoostEnabled = false
        if M.infJumpConnection then M.infJumpConnection:Disconnect(); M.infJumpConnection = nil end
        if M.loopSpeedConnection then M.loopSpeedConnection:Disconnect(); M.loopSpeedConnection = nil end
        if M.loopJumpConnection then M.loopJumpConnection:Disconnect(); M.loopJumpConnection = nil end
        if F.viewConnection then F.viewConnection:Disconnect(); F.viewConnection = nil end
        if F.viewLeaveConnection then F.viewLeaveConnection:Disconnect(); F.viewLeaveConnection = nil end
        if F.viewUpdateConnection then F.viewUpdateConnection:Disconnect(); F.viewUpdateConnection = nil end
        if E.iconEspConnection then E.iconEspConnection:Disconnect(); E.iconEspConnection = nil end
        if E.nameEspConnection then E.nameEspConnection:Disconnect(); E.nameEspConnection = nil end
        if E.stickyPartEspConnection then E.stickyPartEspConnection:Disconnect(); E.stickyPartEspConnection = nil end
        if getgenv().PcldHeartbeatConn then pcall(function() getgenv().PcldHeartbeatConn:Disconnect() end); getgenv().PcldHeartbeatConn = nil end
        getgenv().PcldOwnerRunning = false
        pcall(function() local f = workspace:FindFirstChild("PCLD"); if f then f:Destroy() end end)
        getgenv()._pcldUsedNames = nil
        if L.autoResetDiedConnection then L.autoResetDiedConnection:Disconnect(); L.autoResetDiedConnection = nil end
        if L.autoResetCharConnection then L.autoResetCharConnection:Disconnect(); L.autoResetCharConnection = nil end
        if L.loopresetDiedConnection then L.loopresetDiedConnection:Disconnect(); L.loopresetDiedConnection = nil end
        if L.loopresetCharConnection then L.loopresetCharConnection:Disconnect(); L.loopresetCharConnection = nil end
        if M.spinConnection then M.spinConnection:Disconnect(); M.spinConnection = nil end; M.spinEnabled = false
        if M.spinGrabConnection then M.spinGrabConnection:Disconnect(); M.spinGrabConnection = nil end
        if M.spinGrabWatcher then M.spinGrabWatcher:Disconnect(); M.spinGrabWatcher = nil end
        if M.spinGrabCurrentPart and M.spinGrabCurrentPart.Parent then M.spinGrabCurrentPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) end
        M.spinGrabCurrentPart = nil; M.spinGrabEnabled = false
        if M.noclipConnection then M.noclipConnection:Disconnect(); M.noclipConnection = nil end
        if M.tpwalkConnection then M.tpwalkConnection:Disconnect(); M.tpwalkConnection = nil end; M.tpwalkEnabled = false
        if M.flyConnection then M.flyConnection:Disconnect(); M.flyConnection = nil end
        if L.loopGotoConnection then task.cancel(L.loopGotoConnection); L.loopGotoConnection = nil end
        if M.orbitConnection1 then M.orbitConnection1:Disconnect(); M.orbitConnection1 = nil end
        if M.orbitConnection2 then M.orbitConnection2:Disconnect(); M.orbitConnection2 = nil end
        if M.orbitConnection3 then M.orbitConnection3:Disconnect(); M.orbitConnection3 = nil end
        if M.orbitTargetDiedConn then M.orbitTargetDiedConn:Disconnect(); M.orbitTargetDiedConn = nil end
        if M.orbitTargetRespawnConn then M.orbitTargetRespawnConn:Disconnect(); M.orbitTargetRespawnConn = nil end
        M.orbitEnabled = false
        M.orbitTarget = nil

        pcall(function()
            if S.savedLightingSettings and next(S.savedLightingSettings) then
                for k, v in pairs(S.savedLightingSettings) do Lighting[k] = v end
                S.savedLightingSettings = {}
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if S.originalLightingState and next(S.originalLightingState) then
                for k, v in pairs(S.originalLightingState) do Lighting[k] = v end
                S.originalLightingState = {}
            end
        end)
        S.cachedParts = {}

        F.viewingPlayer = nil
        lp.CameraMode = defaultCameraMode
        lp.CameraMinZoomDistance = defaultMinZoom
        lp.CameraMaxZoomDistance = defaultMaxZoom
        if M.fovLockConnection then M.fovLockConnection:Disconnect(); M.fovLockConnection = nil end; if M.fovLockCameraConnection then M.fovLockCameraConnection:Disconnect(); M.fovLockCameraConnection = nil end; M.fovLockValue = nil
        workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = defaultFOV
        if lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
            workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = lp.Character.Humanoid
        end

        F.friendCache = {}

        local guisToDestroy = {
            serverInfoGui, (getfenv() :: any).helpGuiGlobal, (getfenv() :: any).helpFrame, friendAlertGui, F.friendListGui
        }
        for _, gui in ipairs(guisToDestroy) do
            if gui then pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end) end
        end
        serverInfoGui, (getfenv() :: any).helpGuiGlobal, helpFrame, friendAlertGui, F.friendListGui = nil, nil, nil, nil, nil

        local guiNames = {
            "ServerInfoGui", "HelpGui", "CmdBarGui", "FriendNotification",
            "FriendListGui", "PingNotification", "FriendAlerts", "DaNotifGui",
            "DShubAdminLabel", "TargetInfoGui"
        }
        for _, gui in ipairs(lp.PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do
            if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") then
                for _, name in ipairs(guiNames) do
                    if gui.Name == name then
                        pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end)
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        pcall(getgenv().__DSTD)
        print("스크립트가 완전히 정상적으로 종료되었습니다.")
        return
    end
end

for cmdStr, cmdPlaceId in pairs(AutoExecConfig.CustomCommands) do
    if cmdPlaceId == game.PlaceId then
        getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function()
                processCommand("." .. cmdStr)
            end)
        end))
    end
end

    "",

local HELP_TEXT = table.concat(HELP_TEXT_LINES, "\n")

local CMD_SUGGESTIONS = {}
do
    local seen = {}
    for line in HELP_TEXT:gmatch("[^\r\n]+") do
        local cmdPart, desc = line:match("^%s*•%s*(.-)%s*:%s*(.*)$")
        if cmdPart then
            for piece in cmdPart:gmatch("[^/]+") do
                local cmd = piece:match("(%.[%w가-힣]+)")
                if cmd then
                    local suffix = piece:match("%.[%w가-힣]+%s*(%-[%w가-힣]+)")
                    if suffix then
                        cmd = cmd .. " " .. suffix
                    end
                    if not seen[cmd] then
                        seen[cmd] = true
                        table.insert(CMD_SUGGESTIONS, {cmd = cmd, desc = desc or ""})
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if not seen[".help"] then
        table.insert(CMD_SUGGESTIONS, 1, {cmd = ".help", desc = "명령어 목록 GUI 열기"})
        seen[".help"] = true
    end
    if not seen[".rj no"] then
        table.insert(CMD_SUGGESTIONS, {cmd = ".rj no", desc = "서버 재접속 후 스크 재실행 X"})
        seen[".rj no"] = true
    end
    for _, entry in ipairs(CMD_SUGGESTIONS) do
        local baseCmd = entry.cmd:match("^(%S+)")
        if baseCmd then
            VALID_COMMANDS_SET[baseCmd:sub(2):lower()] = true
        end
    end
end

local function findPlayerMatches(query, maxCount)
    query = tostring(query or ""):lower()
    local results = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= lp then
            local pName, pDisplay = p.Name:lower(), p.DisplayName:lower()
            if #query == 0 or pName:sub(1, #query) == query or pDisplay:sub(1, #query) == query then
                table.insert(results, p)
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(results, function(a, b) return a.DisplayName:lower() < b.DisplayName:lower() end)
    if maxCount and #results > maxCount then
        local trimmed = {}
        for i = 1, maxCount do trimmed[i] = results[i] end
        results = trimmed
    end
    return results
end

local function createHelpGui()
    local screenGui = getgenv().__DSN("ScreenGui")
    screenGui.Name = "HelpGui"
    screenGui.Parent = lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    screenGui.DisplayOrder = 999999999
    screenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true

    local frame = getgenv().__DSN("Frame")
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 360, 0, 500)
    frame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -390, 0.5, -250)
    frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30)
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.Visible = false
    frame.Parent = screenGui
    makeDraggable(frame)
    local corner = getgenv().__DSN("UICorner"); corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10); corner.Parent = frame
    local title = getgenv().__DSN("TextLabel")
    title.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
    title.Text = "명령어 목록"
    title.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    title.TextSize = 18
    title.Parent = frame

    local scrollFrame = getgenv().__DSN("ScrollingFrame")
    scrollFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -60)
    scrollFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 50)
    scrollFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    scrollFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    scrollFrame.ScrollBarThickness = 8
    scrollFrame.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
    scrollFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
    scrollFrame.Parent = frame
    local content = getgenv().__DSN("Frame")
    content.Name = "Content"
    content.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
    content.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 0)
    content.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    content.BorderSizePixel = 0
    content.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    content.Parent = scrollFrame
    local listLayout = getgenv().__DSN("UIListLayout")
    listLayout.Parent = content
    listLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    listLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)

    local helpText = HELP_TEXT

    local isFtapGame = game.PlaceId == 6961824067
    local skipFtapLines = false
    for line in helpText:gmatch("[^\n]+") do
        if not isFtapGame and line:find("FTAP 관련 명령어", 1, true) then
            skipFtapLines = true
        end
        if not skipFtapLines then
            local lbl = getgenv().__DSN("TextLabel")
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
            lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            lbl.TextSize = 14
            lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220,220,220)
            lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            lbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
            lbl.TextWrapped = true
            lbl.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
            lbl.Text = line
            lbl.Parent = content
        end
    end

    local function updateCanvasSize()
        local contentHeight = listLayout.AbsoluteContentSize and listLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y or 0
        scrollFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.ceil(contentHeight + 12))
    end
    getgenv().__DSTH(task.delay(0.03, updateCanvasSize))
    getgenv().__DST(listLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(updateCanvasSize))
    getgenv().__DST(content:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(updateCanvasSize))

    local closeBtn = getgenv().__DSN("TextButton")
    closeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 30)
    closeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -35, 0, 5)
    closeBtn.Text = "X"
    closeBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    closeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 60, 60)
    closeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    closeBtn.Parent = frame
    local btnCorner = getgenv().__DSN("UICorner"); btnCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5); btnCorner.Parent = closeBtn
    getgenv().__DST(closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() frame.Visible = false end))
    return frame, screenGui
end

;(getfenv() :: any).createServerInfoGui = function()
    if serverInfoGui and serverInfoGui.Parent then serverInfoGui.Enabled = not serverInfoGui.Enabled return end
    serverInfoGui = getgenv().__DSN("ScreenGui")
    serverInfoGui.Name = "ServerInfoGui"
    serverInfoGui.Parent = lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    serverInfoGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    serverInfoGui.DisplayOrder = 999999995

    local mainFrame = getgenv().__DSN("Frame")
    mainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 420, 0, 220)
    mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.55, -210, 0.5, -110)
    mainFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18)
    mainFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    mainFrame.Parent = serverInfoGui
    makeDraggable(mainFrame)
    local mainCorner = getgenv().__DSN("UICorner"); mainCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12); mainCorner.Parent = mainFrame

    local header = getgenv().__DSN("Frame")
    header.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
    header.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    header.Parent = mainFrame
    local title = getgenv().__DSN("TextLabel")
    title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 36)
    title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 2)
    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title.Text = "서버 정보"
    title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    title.TextSize = 20
    title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    title.Parent = header

    local closeBtn = getgenv().__DSN("TextButton")
    closeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 30)
    closeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -38, 0, 5)
    closeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 50, 50)
    closeBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    closeBtn.Text = "X"
    closeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    closeBtn.TextSize = 16
    closeBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    closeBtn.AutoButtonColor = true
    closeBtn.ZIndex = 10
    closeBtn.Parent = mainFrame
    local closeBtnCorner = getgenv().__DSN("UICorner")
    closeBtnCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    closeBtnCorner.Parent = closeBtn
    getgenv().__DST(closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        serverInfoGui.Enabled = false
    end))
    local sep = getgenv().__DSN("Frame")
    sep.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 1)
    sep.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 42)
    sep.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 70)
    sep.BorderSizePixel = 0
    sep.Parent = mainFrame

    local playersCurrentLabel = getgenv().__DSN("TextLabel")
    playersCurrentLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -15, 0, 24)
    playersCurrentLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 3, 0, 52)
    playersCurrentLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    playersCurrentLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    playersCurrentLabel.TextSize = 15
    playersCurrentLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230)
    playersCurrentLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    playersCurrentLabel.Text = "현재 인원: " .. tostring(#Players:GetPlayers())
    playersCurrentLabel.Parent = mainFrame

    local playersMaxLabel = getgenv().__DSN("TextLabel")
    playersMaxLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -15, 0, 24)
    playersMaxLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 52)
    playersMaxLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    playersMaxLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    playersMaxLabel.TextSize = 15
    playersMaxLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230)
    playersMaxLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    playersMaxLabel.Parent = mainFrame

    local function createRow(yOffset, labelText, valueText)
        local leftLabel = getgenv().__DSN("TextLabel")
        leftLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.45, -10, 0, 26)
        leftLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, yOffset)
        leftLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        leftLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        leftLabel.TextSize = 14
        leftLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
        leftLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        leftLabel.Text = labelText
        leftLabel.Parent = mainFrame
        local valueLabel = getgenv().__DSN("TextLabel")
        valueLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.35, -10, 0, 26)
        valueLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.45, 0, 0, yOffset)
        valueLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        valueLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        valueLabel.TextSize = 14
        valueLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230)
        valueLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        valueLabel.Text = valueText
        valueLabel.Parent = mainFrame
        local copyBtn = getgenv().__DSN("TextButton")
        copyBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.15, -10, 0, 24)
        copyBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.8, 0, 0, yOffset)
        copyBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
        copyBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        copyBtn.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        copyBtn.TextSize = 13
        copyBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        copyBtn.Text = "Copy"
        copyBtn.AutoButtonColor = true
        copyBtn.Parent = mainFrame
        local btnCorner = getgenv().__DSN("UICorner"); btnCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6); btnCorner.Parent = copyBtn
        return leftLabel, valueLabel, copyBtn
    end
    local _, _, placeCopy = createRow(86, "Place id:", tostring(game.PlaceId))
    getgenv().__DST(placeCopy.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() pcall(function() setclipboard(tostring(game.PlaceId)) end) StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ 복사됨 ]", Text = "Place id가 복사되었습니다.", Duration = 2}) end))
    local _, _, jobCopy = createRow(116, "Job id:", tostring(game.JobId))
    getgenv().__DST(jobCopy.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() pcall(function() setclipboard(tostring(game.JobId)) end) StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ 복사됨 ]", Text = "Job id가 복사되었습니다.", Duration = 2}) end))
    local success, gameInfo = pcall(function() return MarketplaceService:GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId) end)
    local gameNameStr = "Unknown"
    if success and gameInfo and type(gameInfo.Name) == "string" then gameNameStr = gameInfo.Name else if game.PlaceId and tostring(game.PlaceId) ~= "" then gameNameStr = "Place " .. tostring(game.PlaceId) end end
    local _, _, nameCopy = createRow(146, "Game Name:", gameNameStr)
    getgenv().__DST(nameCopy.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() pcall(function() setclipboard(tostring(gameNameStr)) end) StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ 복사됨 ]", Text = "GameName이 복사되었습니다.", Duration = 2}) end))

    local uptimeLabel = getgenv().__DSN("TextLabel")
    uptimeLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 20)
    uptimeLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 176)
    uptimeLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    uptimeLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    uptimeLabel.TextSize = 13
    uptimeLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200,200,200)
    uptimeLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    uptimeLabel.Text = "업타임: 0초"
    uptimeLabel.Parent = mainFrame

    local lastUpdate, updateConn = 0, nil
    updateConn = getgenv().__DST(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        lastUpdate = lastUpdate + dt
        if lastUpdate < 0.5 then return end
        lastUpdate = 0
        if not mainFrame or not mainFrame.Parent then if updateConn then updateConn:Disconnect() updateConn = nil end return end
        playersCurrentLabel.Text = "현재 인원: " .. tostring(#Players:GetPlayers())
        local elapsed = math.floor(tick() - startTime)
        local h = math.floor(elapsed / 3600); local m = math.floor((elapsed % 3600) / 60); local s = elapsed % 60
        uptimeLabel.Text = string.format("업타임: %02d:%02d:%02d", h, m, s)
    end))
    return mainFrame
end

local function showFriendAlert(text, kind)
    if not friendAlertGui or not friendAlertGui.Parent then
        friendAlertGui = getgenv().__DSN("ScreenGui")
        friendAlertGui.Name = "FriendAlerts"
        friendAlertGui.Parent = lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
        friendAlertGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        friendAlertGui.DisplayOrder = 999999999
    end

    kind = tostring(kind or ""):lower()
    local alertWidth, alertHeight, frame = 300, 60, getgenv().__DSN("Frame")
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, alertWidth, 0, alertHeight)

    frame.Position = UDim2.new(1, 20, 0.5, 0)
    frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)

    frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.Parent = friendAlertGui

    local corner = getgenv().__DSN("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
    corner.Parent = frame

    local stroke = getgenv().__DSN("UIStroke")
    stroke.Parent = frame
    stroke.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Round
    stroke.Thickness = 3

    if kind == "join" then
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(50, 255, 50)
    elseif kind == "leave" then
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
    elseif kind == "da" then
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 180, 255)
    else
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    end

    local lbl = getgenv().__DSN("TextLabel")
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -30, 1, 0)
    lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    lbl.TextSize = 16
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    lbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    lbl.TextWrapped = true
    lbl.Text = text
    lbl.Parent = frame

    local offsetIndex = #activeAlerts
    local offsetY = (offsetIndex * (alertHeight + 10))

    local visiblePos, hiddenPos, ti =
        UDim2.new(1, -20, 0.5, -offsetY),
        UDim2.new(1, alertWidth + 50, 0.5, -offsetY),
        TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    TweenService:Create(frame, ti, {Position = visiblePos}):Play()

    table.insert(activeAlerts, frame)

    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(4)
        local out = TweenService:Create(frame, ti, {Position = hiddenPos})
        out:Play()
        out.Completed:Wait()
        for i,v in ipairs(activeAlerts) do
            if v == frame then table.remove(activeAlerts, i) break end
        end
        pcall(function() frame:Destroy() end)
    end))
end

local COMMAND_TOGGLE_MAP = {
    fly = { onCmd = ".fly", offCmd = ".unfly", label = "Fly", icon = "F",
        getState = function() return M.flyConnection ~= nil end },
    noclip = { onCmd = ".noclip", offCmd = ".unnoclip", label = "Noclip", icon = "N",
        getState = function() return M.noclipConnection ~= nil end },
    antilag = { onCmd = ".antilag", offCmd = ".unantilag", label = "Anti Lag", icon = "A",
        getState = function() return A.charAntiLagEnabled == true end },
}

local CMD_TYPE_SOUND_IDS = { -- 이 목록에 있는 소리 중 하나가 랜덤으로 재생됩니다
    "rbxassetid://96591611478915",
}
local CMD_TYPE_SOUND_POOL = {}
local CMD_TYPE_SOUND_POOL_SIZE = 5
local CMD_TYPE_SOUND_POOL_INDEX = 0
do
    local soundService = game:GetService("SoundService")
    for i = 1, CMD_TYPE_SOUND_POOL_SIZE do
        local s = getgenv().__DSN("Sound")
        s.Name = "CmdTypeSound"
        s.Volume = 3
        s.Parent = soundService
        CMD_TYPE_SOUND_POOL[i] = s
    end
end
local function playCmdTypeSound()
    CMD_TYPE_SOUND_POOL_INDEX = (CMD_TYPE_SOUND_POOL_INDEX % CMD_TYPE_SOUND_POOL_SIZE) + 1
    local s = CMD_TYPE_SOUND_POOL[CMD_TYPE_SOUND_POOL_INDEX]
    local chosenId = CMD_TYPE_SOUND_IDS[math.random(1, #CMD_TYPE_SOUND_IDS)]
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        s.SoundId = chosenId
        s:Play()
    end)
    if not ok then
        warn("[CmdTypeSound] 재생 실패 (" .. tostring(chosenId) .. "): " .. tostring(err))
    end
end

    suggestBox.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    suggestBox.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    suggestBox.ScrollBarThickness = 5

        local rowStroke = getgenv().__DSN("UIStroke")
