        end))
        getgenv().__DST(row.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.75
            pcall(function() TweenService:Create(rowScale, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Scale = 1}):Play() end)
        end))

        suggestRows[i] = { row = row, icon = iconLabel, label = label, dot = dot, data = nil }
    end

    local function hideSuggestions()
        suggestBox.Visible = false
        suggestBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 720, 0, 0)
        for _, r in ipairs(suggestRows) do
            r.row.Visible = false
            r.data = nil
        end
    end

    -- rows: 리스트 { {cmd, desc, fillText(선택), toggleKey(선택)} , ... }
    local function renderRows(rows)
        if #rows == 0 then
            for i, r in ipairs(suggestRows) do
                if i == 1 then
                    r.row.Visible = true
                    r.icon.Text = "!"
                    r.label.Text = "<font color=\"#B0B0B8\">일치하는 명령어가 없습니다</font>"
                    r.dot.Visible = false
                    r.data = nil
                else
                    r.row.Visible = false
                    r.data = nil
                end
            end
            suggestBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 720, 0, 46 + 16)
            suggestBox.Visible = true
            suggestBox.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
            return
        end
        for i, r in ipairs(suggestRows) do
            local item = rows[i]
            if item then
                local toggleInfo = item.toggleKey and COMMAND_TOGGLE_MAP[item.toggleKey]
                r.row.Visible = true
                r.label.Text = string.format("<b>%s</b>  <font color=\"#9FEAD9\">%s</font>", item.display or item.cmd, item.desc or "")
                if toggleInfo then
                    r.icon.Text = toggleInfo.icon
                else
                    r.icon.Text = item.cmd:sub(2, 2):upper()
                end
                r.dot.Visible = false
                r.data = item
            else
                r.row.Visible = false
                r.data = nil
            end
        end
        local visibleCount = math.min(#rows, MAX_SUGGESTIONS)
        local ROW_H, ROW_PAD, BOX_PAD = 46, 3, 16
        local contentH = visibleCount * ROW_H + math.max(visibleCount - 1, 0) * ROW_PAD + BOX_PAD
        suggestBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 720, 0, math.min(contentH, SUGGEST_MAX_HEIGHT))
        suggestBox.Visible = true
        suggestBox.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    end

    local function updateSuggestions(text)
        text = tostring(text or "")
        if text:sub(1,1) == "/" then hideSuggestions(); return end

        local spacePos = text:find("%s")
        if spacePos then
            local cmdWord = text:sub(1, spacePos - 1):gsub("^%.", ""):lower()

            if ENUM_ARG_COMMANDS[cmdWord] then
                local argText = text:sub(spacePos + 1)
                local query = argText:match("([^,]*)$") or ""
                query = query:gsub("^%s+", ""):lower()

                local options = ENUM_ARG_COMMANDS[cmdWord]
                local optMatches = {}
                for _, opt in ipairs(options) do
                    if opt:sub(1, #query) == query then
                        table.insert(optMatches, opt)
                        if #optMatches >= MAX_SUGGESTIONS then break end
                    end
                end
                if #optMatches == 0 then renderRows({}); return end

                local prefixBeforeQuery = text:sub(1, spacePos) .. argText:sub(1, #argText - #query)
                local rows = {}
                for _, opt in ipairs(optMatches) do
                    local fillText = prefixBeforeQuery .. opt
                    local desc = ENUM_ARG_DESC[opt] or ""
                    if cmdWord == "disable" then
                        desc = desc:gsub("활성화", "비활성화")
                    end
                    local display = nil
                    if cmdWord == "ovenall" and #query > 0 and #query <= #opt then
                        display = prefixBeforeQuery
                            .. "<font color=\"#FFD84D\">" .. opt:sub(1, #query) .. "</font>"
                            .. opt:sub(#query + 1)
                    end
                    table.insert(rows, { cmd = fillText, display = display, desc = desc, fillText = fillText })
                end
                renderRows(rows)
                return
            end

            local AUTOEXE_CMD_WORDS = { autoexe = true, autoexecute = true, unautoexe = true, unautoexecute = true }
            if AUTOEXE_CMD_WORDS[cmdWord] then
                local argText = text:sub(spacePos + 1)
                local query = argText:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("^%.", ""):lower()

                if #query == 0 then hideSuggestions(); return end

                local cmdMatches = {}
                for _, entry in ipairs(CMD_SUGGESTIONS) do
                    local cmdBody = entry.cmd:sub(2):lower()
                    if cmdBody:find(query, 1, true) then
                        table.insert(cmdMatches, entry)
                        if #cmdMatches >= MAX_SUGGESTIONS then break end
                    end
                end
                if #cmdMatches == 0 then renderRows({}); return end

                local prefixBeforeQuery = text:sub(1, spacePos)
                local rows = {}
                for _, entry in ipairs(cmdMatches) do
                    local fillText = prefixBeforeQuery .. entry.cmd:sub(2)
                    table.insert(rows, { cmd = fillText, desc = entry.desc, fillText = fillText })
                end
                renderRows(rows)
                return
            end

            if NAME_ARG_COMMANDS[cmdWord] then
                local argText = text:sub(spacePos + 1)
                local query = argText:match("([^,]*)$") or ""
                query = query:gsub("^%s+", "")

                local players = findPlayerMatches(query, MAX_SUGGESTIONS)
                if #players == 0 then renderRows({}); return end

                local prefixBeforeQuery = text:sub(1, spacePos) .. argText:sub(1, #argText - #query)
                local rows = {}
                for _, p in ipairs(players) do
                    local fillText = prefixBeforeQuery .. p.DisplayName
                    local display = fillText
                    if #query > 0 and #query <= #p.DisplayName then
                        display = prefixBeforeQuery
                            .. "<font color=\"#FFD84D\">" .. p.DisplayName:sub(1, #query) .. "</font>"
                            .. p.DisplayName:sub(#query + 1)
                    end
                    table.insert(rows, { cmd = fillText, display = display, desc = "( @" .. p.Name .. " | ID: " .. tostring(p.UserId) .. " )", fillText = fillText })
                end
                renderRows(rows)
                return
            end
        end

        local typed = text:gsub("^%.", ""):lower()

        if #typed == 0 then
            -- 입력창이 비어있을 때: 전체 명령어 목록을 알파벳 순으로 표시 (토글형 명령어는 대표 항목 하나로 합침)
            local skipBody = {}
            for key, info in pairs(COMMAND_TOGGLE_MAP) do
                skipBody[info.offCmd:sub(2):lower()] = true
            end
            local sortedEntries = {}
            for _, entry in ipairs(CMD_SUGGESTIONS) do
                local bodyKey = entry.cmd:sub(2):lower()
                if not skipBody[bodyKey] then
                    table.insert(sortedEntries, entry)
                end
            end
            table.sort(sortedEntries, function(a, b) return a.cmd:sub(2):lower() < b.cmd:sub(2):lower() end)
            local rows = {}
            for _, entry in ipairs(sortedEntries) do
                local bodyKey = entry.cmd:sub(2):lower()
                local toggleKey = nil
                for key, info in pairs(COMMAND_TOGGLE_MAP) do
                    if info.onCmd:sub(2):lower() == bodyKey then toggleKey = key break end
                end
                table.insert(rows, { cmd = entry.cmd, desc = entry.desc, toggleKey = toggleKey })
                if #rows >= MAX_SUGGESTIONS then break end
            end
            renderRows(rows)
            return
        end

        local matches = {}
        for _, entry in ipairs(CMD_SUGGESTIONS) do
            local cmdBody = entry.cmd:sub(2):lower()
            local s = cmdBody:find(typed, 1, true)
            if s then
                local score
                if cmdBody == typed then
                    score = 0
                elseif s == 1 then
                    score = 1000 + (#cmdBody - #typed)
                else
                    score = 2000 + s * 10 + (#cmdBody - #typed)
                end
                table.insert(matches, { entry = entry, cmdBody = cmdBody, score = score })
            end
        end

        if #matches == 0 then renderRows({}); return end

        table.sort(matches, function(a, b)
            if a.score ~= b.score then return a.score < b.score end
            return a.cmdBody < b.cmdBody
        end)

        local rows = {}
        for i, m in ipairs(matches) do
            if i > MAX_SUGGESTIONS then break end
            local entry = m.entry
            local bodyKey = m.cmdBody
            local toggleKey = nil
            for key, info in pairs(COMMAND_TOGGLE_MAP) do
                if info.onCmd:sub(2):lower() == bodyKey then toggleKey = key break end
            end

            local display = entry.cmd
            local ms, me = bodyKey:find(typed, 1, true)
            if ms then
                display = entry.cmd:sub(1, ms) .. "<font color=\"#FFD84D\">" .. entry.cmd:sub(ms + 1, me + 1) .. "</font>" .. entry.cmd:sub(me + 2)
            end

            table.insert(rows, { cmd = entry.cmd, display = display, desc = entry.desc, toggleKey = toggleKey })
        end
        renderRows(rows)
    end

    local showBar, hideBar, closingBar
    closingBar = false

    showBar = function()
        bg.Position = showPos
        bg.GroupTransparency = 1
        bg.Visible = true
        outsideCatcher.Visible = true
        pcall(function() TweenService:Create(bg, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {GroupTransparency = 0}):Play() end)
        updateSuggestions(input.Text)
    end

    hideBar = function()
        closingBar = true
        hideSuggestions()
        pcall(function() input:ReleaseFocus() end)
        pcall(function()
            local tw = TweenService:Create(bg, TweenInfo.new(0.24, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Position = hideUpPos, GroupTransparency = 1})
            tw:Play(); tw.Completed:Wait()
        end)
        bg.Visible = false
        bg.Position = showPos
        bg.GroupTransparency = 1
        outsideCatcher.Visible = false
        input.Text = ""
        task.wait()
        hideSuggestions()
        F.cmdFocused = false
        closingBar = false
    end

    for _, r in ipairs(suggestRows) do
        getgenv().__DST(r.row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local data = r.data
            if not data then return end
            if data.toggleKey and COMMAND_TOGGLE_MAP[data.toggleKey] then
                local info = COMMAND_TOGGLE_MAP[data.toggleKey]
                local isOn = false
                pcall(function() isOn = info.getState() end)
                local cmdToRun = isOn and info.offCmd or info.onCmd
                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function() processCommand(cmdToRun) end))
                getgenv().__DSTH(task.delay(0.15, function()
                    pcall(function() updateSuggestions(input.Text) end)
                end))
                pcall(function() input:CaptureFocus() end)
            else
                local cmdToRun = data.fillText or data.cmd
                if cmdToRun:sub(1,1) ~= "." then cmdToRun = "." .. cmdToRun end
                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(hideBar))
                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function() processCommand(cmdToRun) end))
            end
        end))
    end

    getgenv().__DST(input:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        if closingBar then return end
        playCmdTypeSound()
        updateSuggestions(input.Text)
    end))

    getgenv().__DST(outsideCatcher.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if bg.Visible then getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(hideBar)) end
    end))

    getgenv().__DST(input.Focused:Connect(function() F.cmdFocused = true end))
    getgenv().__DST(input.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
        local txt = tostring(input.Text or "")
        if enterPressed then
            if #txt > 0 then
                input.Text = ""
                hideSuggestions()
                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(hideBar))
                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                    if txt:sub(1,1) == "/" then sendChatMessage(txt:sub(2))
                    else
                        if txt:sub(1,1) ~= "." then txt = "." .. txt end
                        processCommand(txt)
                    end
                end))
            else
                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(hideBar))
            end
        else
            -- 엔터/ESC/외부 클릭이 아닌 포커스 이탈(추천 항목 클릭 등)이면 창을 유지하고 포커스만 재획득
            if not closingBar and bg.Visible then
                getgenv().__DSTH(task.defer(function()
                    if bg.Visible and not closingBar then
                        pcall(function() input:CaptureFocus() end)
                    end
                end))
            end
        end
    end))

    getgenv().__DST(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inputObj, gpe)
        if inputObj.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape and bg.Visible then
            getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(hideBar))
        end
    end))

    return bg, input, showBar, hideBar
end

local function updateFriendStatus(player)
    local success, isFriend = pcall(function()
        return lp:IsFriendsWith(player.UserId)
    end)
    if success and isFriend then
        F.friendCache[player.UserId] = true
    end
end

getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
    local allPlayers, pending, done = Players:GetPlayers(), 0, 0

    for _, p in ipairs(allPlayers) do
        if p ~= lp then
            pending = pending + 1
            getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                updateFriendStatus(p)
                done = done + 1
            end))
        end
    end

    local waited = 0
    while done < pending and waited < 2 do
        task.wait(0.05)
        waited = waited + 0.05
    end

    Z.initialLoadDone = true
    updateFriendList()
end))


getgenv().__DST(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
    if Z.joiningPlayers[player.UserId] then return end
    Z.joiningPlayers[player.UserId] = true

    local success, isFriend = false, false
    for attempt = 1, 3 do
        success, isFriend = pcall(function()
            return lp:IsFriendsWith(player.UserId)
        end)
        if success then break end
        task.wait(0.1)
    end

    if not Players:GetPlayerByUserId(player.UserId) then
        Z.joiningPlayers[player.UserId] = nil
        return
    end

    if success and isFriend then
        if not F.friendCache[player.UserId] then
            F.friendCache[player.UserId] = true
            showFriendAlert(player.DisplayName .. "님이 서버에 접속했습니다!", "join")
            updateFriendList()
        end
    end

    Z.joiningPlayers[player.UserId] = nil
end))

getgenv().__DST(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
    if F.friendCache[player.UserId] then
        F.friendCache[player.UserId] = nil
        showFriendAlert(player.DisplayName .. "님이 서버에서 나갔습니다.", "leave")
        getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(updateFriendList))
    end
end))

local helpFrameGlobal, helpGuiGlobal = createHelpGui()
helpFrame = helpFrameGlobal
local cmdFrame, cmdInput, cmdShow, cmdHide = createCmdBar()

local function CheckNetworkOwner(targetPart)
    local hasOwnership = true

    pcall(function()
        if type((getfenv() :: any).isnetworkowner) == "function" then
            hasOwnership = (getfenv() :: any).isnetworkowner(targetPart)
        end
    end)

    return hasOwnership
end

local targetPlaceId = 6961824067

if game.PlaceId == targetPlaceId then
    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(0.1)
        local character = lp.Character or lp.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if rootPart then
            local _result = CheckNetworkOwner(rootPart)
        end
    end))

    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(0.1)
        if AutoExecConfig.NoclipBarrier then
            toggleNoclipBarrier(true)
        end
        if AutoExecConfig.OceanWalk then
            toggleOceanWalk(true)
        end
        if AutoExecConfig.StickyPartEsp then
            toggleStickyPartEsp(true)
        end

        task.wait(0.1)

        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", { Title = "[ 자동 설정 완료 ]", Text = "특정 게임(ID:"..targetPlaceId..") 설정이 적용되었습니다.", Duration = 5 })
    end))

    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(0.1)
        if AutoExecConfig.AntiInPlots then
            toggleAntiInPlots(true)
        end
    end))

    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(0.1)
        local grabEvents = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GrabEvents", 5)
        if grabEvents then
            local endGrabEarly = grabEvents:WaitForChild("EndGrabEarly", 5)
            if endGrabEarly then
                endGrabEarly:Destroy()
                local dummyEvent = getgenv().__DSN("RemoteEvent")
                dummyEvent.Name = "EndGrabEarly"
                dummyEvent.Parent = grabEvents
            end
        end
    end))
end

print("스크립트 버전 v44.6 실행됨 / .help 명령어를 이용해서 명령어 목록 확인")

pcall(function()
    if game.PlaceId ~= 6961824067 then return end
    local gui = getgenv().__DSN("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "GrabInfoGui"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.DisplayOrder = 50

    local okUi, uiRoot = pcall(function()
        return gethui and gethui()
    end)
    if okUi and uiRoot then
        gui.Parent = uiRoot
    else
        gui.Parent = CoreGui
    end

    local card = getgenv().__DSN("Frame")
    card.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
    card.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -24)
    card.Size = UDim2.new(0, 260, 0, 156)
    card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 24)
    card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    card.BorderSizePixel = 0
    card.Visible = false
    card.Active = false
    card.Parent = gui

    local corner = getgenv().__DSN("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    corner.Parent = card

    local stroke = getgenv().__DSN("UIStroke")
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 110)
    stroke.Thickness = 1
    stroke.Parent = card

    local pad = getgenv().__DSN("UIPadding")
    pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10)
    pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10)
    pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6)
    pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6)
    pad.Parent = card

    local layout = getgenv().__DSN("UIListLayout")
    layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
    layout.Parent = card

    local function makeLabel(order)
        local label = getgenv().__DSN("TextLabel")
        label.LayoutOrder = order
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        label.TextSize = 18
        label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 240)
        label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        label.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        label.Text = ""
        label.Parent = card
        return label
    end

    local ownerLabel = makeLabel(1)
    local partLabel = makeLabel(2)
    local modelLabel = makeLabel(3)
    local anchoredLabel = makeLabel(4)
    local ownLabel = makeLabel(5)
    local massLabel = makeLabel(6)

    local current = nil
    local heldConn = nil
    local ownConn = nil

    local function describe(part)
        local node = part
        while node and node ~= Workspace and node ~= game do
            local parent = node.Parent
            if node:IsA("Model") then
                local pl = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(node)
                if pl then return pl.Name, node.Name end
            end
            if parent and parent.Name:match("SpawnedInToys$") then
                return (parent.Name:gsub("SpawnedInToys$", "")), node.Name
            end
            node = parent
        end
        local model = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
        return "알 수 없음", model and model.Name or part.Name
    end

    local function findToyRoot(part)
        local node = part
        while node and node ~= Workspace and node ~= game do
            local parent = node.Parent
            if parent and parent.Name:match("SpawnedInToys$") then return node end
            if node:IsA("Model") and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(node) then return node end
            node = parent
        end
        local top = nil
        local m = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
        while m do
            top = m
            m = m:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
        end
        return top or part
    end

    local function collectParts(root, name)
        local parts = {}
        if root:IsA("BasePart") and (name == nil or root.Name == name) then
            table.insert(parts, root)
        end
        for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("BasePart") and (name == nil or d.Name == name) then
                table.insert(parts, d)
            end
        end
        return parts
    end

    local function scanAnchor(held, root, grabParts)
        if held.Anchored then
            anchoredLabel.Text = "고정 : true"
            return
        end
        for _, part in ipairs(collectParts(root, nil)) do
            if current ~= grabParts then return end
            if part.Anchored then
                anchoredLabel.Text = "고정 : true"
                return
            end
            task.wait()
        end
        if current == grabParts then
            anchoredLabel.Text = "고정 : false"
        end
    end

    local function findCharModel(part)
        local m = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
        while m do
            if m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then return m end
            m = m:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function watchOwner(po, grabParts)
        ownLabel.Text = "소유권 : " .. po.Value
        if ownConn then ownConn:Disconnect() end
        local conn
        conn = getgenv().__DST(po.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
            if parent == nil then
                conn:Disconnect()
                if current == grabParts then
                    ownLabel.Text = "소유권 : 잃음"
                end
            end
        end))
        ownConn = conn
    end

    local function scanOwner(held, root, grabParts)
        local name = held.Name
        local charModel = findCharModel(held)
        for _ = 1, 10 do
            if current ~= grabParts then return end
            if charModel then
                local head = charModel:FindFirstChild("Head")
                local po = head and head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                if po and po:IsA("StringValue") and po.Value ~= "" then
                    watchOwner(po, grabParts)
                    return
                end
            else
                for _, part in ipairs(collectParts(root, name)) do
                    if current ~= grabParts then return end
                    local po = part:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                    if po and po:IsA("StringValue") and po.Value ~= "" then
                        watchOwner(po, grabParts)
                        return
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.3)
        end
        if current == grabParts then
            ownLabel.Text = "소유권 : 없음"
        end
    end

    local function findHeld(grabParts)
        local grabPart = grabParts:WaitForChild("GrabPart", 3)
        if grabPart == nil then return nil end
        for _ = 1, 30 do
            if grabParts.Parent == nil then return nil end
            for _, d in ipairs(grabParts:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
                    local other = d.Part1
                    if other == grabPart then other = d.Part0 end
                    if other and other ~= grabPart then return other end
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function hideCard(grabParts)
        if current == grabParts then
            current = nil
            card.Visible = false
            if heldConn then
                heldConn:Disconnect()
                heldConn = nil
            end
            if ownConn then
                ownConn:Disconnect()
                ownConn = nil
            end
        end
    end

    local function onGrab(grabParts)
        current = grabParts
        getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
            local held = findHeld(grabParts)
            if current ~= grabParts or held == nil then return end
            local owner, model = describe(held)
            ownerLabel.Text = "주인: " .. owner
            partLabel.Text = "파트: " .. held.Name
            modelLabel.Text = "물건: " .. model
            anchoredLabel.Text = "고정 : 확인 중..."
            ownLabel.Text = "소유권 : 확인 중..."
            massLabel.Text = "질량 : " .. string.format("%.2f", held.AssemblyMass)
            local root = findToyRoot(held)
            getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(scanAnchor, held, root, grabParts))
            getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(scanOwner, held, root, grabParts))
            card.Visible = true
            if heldConn then heldConn:Disconnect() end
            heldConn = getgenv().__DST(held.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
                if parent == nil then hideCard(grabParts) end
            end))
        end))
    end

    getgenv().__DST(Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child.Name == "GrabParts" then
            onGrab(child)
        end
    end))

    getgenv().__DST(Workspace.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
        hideCard(child)
    end))

    local existing = Workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")
    if existing then onGrab(existing) end
end)

local RANKS = {
    ["mirimirimirihyuk"] = 1,
    ["saleexpense177"]   = 1,
    [".mirimirimirihyuk1"] = 1,
}
local RANK_LABEL = {
    [1] = " Owner",
    [2] = " Co-Owner",
}

local function getMyRank()
    return RANKS[lp.Name:lower()] or 999
end

local function getSenderRank(senderName)
    return RANKS[senderName:lower()] or 999
end

local function canCommand(senderRank)
    local myRank = getMyRank()
    return senderRank < myRank
end

local function isTargeted(targetName)
    local t = targetName:lower():gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    if t == "all" then return true end
    local myName, myDisplay = lp.Name:lower(), lp.DisplayName:lower()
    return myName:find(t, 1, true) ~= nil or myDisplay:find(t, 1, true) ~= nil
end

local function forceKillSelf()
    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
        local char = lp.Character or lp.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        if char then
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then hum.Health = 0 end
            task.wait(0.1)
            local hum2 = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum2 and hum2.Health > 0 then hum2.Health = 0 end
            local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if root then root.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -1000, 0) end
        end
    end))
end

local function forceBringSelf(commanderPlayer, slotIndex)
    slotIndex = slotIndex or 0
    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
        local cmdChar, myChar = commanderPlayer.Character, lp.Character
        if cmdChar and myChar then
            local cmdRoot, myRoot =
                cmdChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"),
                myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if cmdRoot and myRoot then
                local offsetX = (slotIndex - 0) * 3
                if slotIndex > 0 then
                    local half = math.ceil(slotIndex / 2) * 3
                    if slotIndex % 2 == 1 then
                        offsetX = -half
                    else
                        offsetX = half
                    end
                end
                local targetCFrame = cmdRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(offsetX, 3, -10)
                myRoot.CFrame = targetCFrame
            end
        end
    end))
end

local function forceKickSelf()
    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "[ 킥 당하셨습니다 ]",
                Text = "당신은 오너에 의해서 킥 당하셨습니다.",
                Duration = 5
            })
        end)
        task.wait(1.5)

        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        if LocalPlayer then
            LocalPlayer:Kick("오너에 의해 킥을 당하셨습니다")
        else
            warn("LocalPlayer를 찾을 수 없습니다.")
        end
    end))
end

local function forceRejoinSelf()
    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "[ 리조인 ]",
                Text = "오너에 의해 재접속됩니다.",
                Duration = 3
            })
        end)
        task.wait(1)
        if #Players:GetPlayers() <= 1 then
            TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, lp)
        else
            TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, lp)
        end
    end))
end

local lockState = { active = false, cframe = nil, conn = nil, charConn = nil }

local function forceUnlockSelf()
    lockState.active = false
    if lockState.conn then lockState.conn:Disconnect(); lockState.conn = nil end
    if lockState.charConn then lockState.charConn:Disconnect(); lockState.charConn = nil end
    lockState.cframe = nil
    pcall(function()
        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
            Title = "[ 고정 해제 ]",
            Text = "오너에 의해 고정이 해제되었습니다.",
            Duration = 3
        })
    end)
end

local function forceLockSelf()
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return end
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return end

    lockState.active = true
    lockState.cframe = root.CFrame

    if lockState.conn then lockState.conn:Disconnect() end
    lockState.conn = getgenv().__DST(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not lockState.active then return end
        local c = lp.Character
        local r = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if r and lockState.cframe then
            r.CFrame = lockState.cframe
            r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            r.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end
    end))

    if lockState.charConn then lockState.charConn:Disconnect() end
    lockState.charConn = getgenv().__DST(lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
        if not lockState.active then return end
        local newRoot = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
        if newRoot and lockState.cframe then
            newRoot.CFrame = lockState.cframe
        end
    end))

    pcall(function()
        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
            Title = "[ 고정됨 ]",
            Text = "오너에 의해 현재 위치에 고정되었습니다.",
            Duration = 5
        })
    end)
end


local function forceLagSelf(duration)
    duration = duration or 1
    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
        local playerScripts = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
        if not playerScripts then return end
        local fireAllRemotes = playerScripts:FindFirstChild("[ExploitTest]FireAllRemotes")
        if not fireAllRemotes then return end

        local endTime = tick() + duration
        while tick() < endTime do
            pcall(function() fireAllRemotes.Disabled = false end)
            task.wait()
            pcall(function() fireAllRemotes.Disabled = false end)
            pcall(function() fireAllRemotes.Disabled = false end)
            pcall(function() fireAllRemotes.Disabled = false end)
        end

        pcall(function() fireAllRemotes.Disabled = true end)
    end))
end

local function isTargetingOwner(targetName)
    local t = targetName:lower():gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    for name, rank in pairs(RANKS) do
        if rank == 1 then
            if name:find(t, 1, true) then return true end
        end
    end
    return false
end

local function handleRankCommand(senderPlayer, msg)
    local senderRank, myRank = getSenderRank(senderPlayer.Name), getMyRank()

    if senderRank == 999 then return end

    local cleanMsg = msg:gsub("^%s+", ""):lower()

    local function stripDash(s)
        if s:sub(1, 1) == "-" then return s:sub(2) else return s end
    end
    local normMsg = stripDash(cleanMsg)

    if senderRank == 2 and normMsg:sub(1, 5) == "kill " then
        local offset = (cleanMsg:sub(1, 1) == "-") and 7 or 6
        local targetName = msg:sub(offset):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        if isTargetingOwner(targetName) then
            if lp.Name:lower() == senderPlayer.Name:lower() then
                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                    task.wait(0.2)
                    forceKillSelf()
                end))
            end
            return
        end
    end

    if senderRank >= 2 and myRank == 1 then return end

    if not canCommand(senderRank) then return end

    if normMsg:sub(1, 5) == "kill " then
        local offset = (cleanMsg:sub(1, 1) == "-") and 7 or 6
        local targetName = msg:sub(offset):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        if isTargeted(targetName) then
            forceKillSelf()
        end

    elseif normMsg:sub(1, 6) == "bring " then
        local offset = (cleanMsg:sub(1, 1) == "-") and 8 or 7
        local targetName = msg:sub(offset):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        if isTargeted(targetName) then
            local slotKey = senderPlayer.UserId
            if not Z.bringAllSlots[slotKey] then Z.bringAllSlots[slotKey] = 0 end
            local mySlot = Z.bringAllSlots[slotKey]
            Z.bringAllSlots[slotKey] = Z.bringAllSlots[slotKey] + 1
            getgenv().__DSTH(task.delay(mySlot * 0.05, function()
                forceBringSelf(senderPlayer, mySlot)
            end))
            getgenv().__DSTH(task.delay(0.5, function()
                Z.bringAllSlots[slotKey] = nil
            end))
        end

    elseif normMsg:sub(1, 5) == "kick " then
        local offset = (cleanMsg:sub(1, 1) == "-") and 7 or 6
        local targetName = msg:sub(offset):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        if isTargeted(targetName) then
            forceKickSelf()
        end

    elseif normMsg:sub(1, 4) == "lag " then
        local offset = (cleanMsg:sub(1, 1) == "-") and 6 or 5
        local args = msg:sub(offset):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        local targetName, duration
        local namePart, numPart = args:match("^(.-)%s+(%d+%.?%d*)%s*$")
        if namePart and numPart then
            targetName = namePart
            duration = tonumber(numPart)
        else
            targetName = args
            duration = 3
        end
        if isTargeted(targetName) then
            forceLagSelf(duration)
        end

    elseif normMsg:sub(1, 3) == "rj " then
        local offset = (cleanMsg:sub(1, 1) == "-") and 5 or 4
        local targetName = msg:sub(offset):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        if isTargeted(targetName) then
            forceRejoinSelf()
        end

    elseif normMsg:sub(1, 5) == "lock " or normMsg == "lock" then
        local targetName
        if normMsg == "lock" then
            targetName = "all"
        else
            local offset = (cleanMsg:sub(1, 1) == "-") and 7 or 6
            targetName = msg:sub(offset):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        end
        if isTargeted(targetName) then
            forceLockSelf()
        end

    elseif normMsg:sub(1, 7) == "unlock " or normMsg == "unlock" then
        local targetName
        if normMsg == "unlock" then
            targetName = "all"
        else
            local offset = (cleanMsg:sub(1, 1) == "-") and 9 or 8
            targetName = msg:sub(offset):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        end
        if isTargeted(targetName) then
            forceUnlockSelf()
        end

    elseif normMsg == "da" then
        if senderRank == 1 and myRank ~= 1 then
            getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                pcall(function()
                    local tcs = game:GetService("TextChatService")
                    local channel = tcs:FindFirstChild("TextChannels")
                        and tcs.TextChannels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
                    if channel then
                        channel:SendAsync("em.")
                    end
                end)
            end))
        end

    elseif normMsg:sub(1, 4) == "off " or normMsg == "off" then
        if senderRank == 1 and myRank ~= 1 then
            local rawArg = ""
            if normMsg == "off" then
                rawArg = ""
            else
                rawArg = normMsg:sub(5):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
            end

            local shouldOff = false

            if rawArg == "all" or rawArg == "" then
                shouldOff = true
            else
                for namePart in rawArg:gmatch("[^,]+") do
                    namePart = namePart:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
                    local myName, myDisplay = lp.Name:lower(), lp.DisplayName:lower()
                    if myName:find(namePart, 1, true) or myDisplay:find(namePart, 1, true) then
                        shouldOff = true
                        break
                    end
                end
            end

            if shouldOff then
                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    _scriptTerminated = true
                    getgenv().DShubScript = false
                    pcall(Z.ShutdownCommands)
                    pcall(function()
                        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", { Title = "[ 스크립트 종료 ]", Text = "오너 명령에 의해 스크립트가 강제 종료됩니다.", Duration = 4 })
                        if chatConnection then chatConnection:Disconnect(); chatConnection = nil end
                        if mainInputConnection then mainInputConnection:Disconnect(); mainInputConnection = nil end
                        toggleAntiLag(false)
                        toggleFpsBoost(false)
                        A.antiAfkEnabled = false
                        if A.antiAfkConnection then A.antiAfkConnection:Disconnect(); A.antiAfkConnection = nil end
                        if A.antiAfkLoop then task.cancel(A.antiAfkLoop); A.antiAfkLoop = nil end
                        stopFlying()
                        toggleNoclip(false)
                        M.walkflingEnabled = false
                        toggleSpin(false)
                        if getgenv then
                            getgenv().PcldOwnerRunning = false
                            if getgenv().ForceStopPcldLoop then
                                pcall(function() getgenv().ForceStopPcldLoop:Disconnect() end)
                                getgenv().ForceStopPcldLoop = nil
                            end
                        end
                        toggleAutoReset(false)
                        stopLoopKill()
                        toggleNameEsp(false)
                        A.charAntiLagEnabled = false
                        if A.autoAntiLagLoop then task.cancel(A.autoAntiLagLoop); A.autoAntiLagLoop = nil end
                        if M.voidTpEnabled then M.voidTpEnabled = false; AntiGrabTP_ActiveV1 = false end
                        if M.loopTpEnabled then M.loopTpEnabled = false; AntiGrabTP_ActiveV2 = false end
                        if L.lpwdEnabled then
                            L.lpwdEnabled = false
                            if L.lpwdLoop then task.cancel(L.lpwdLoop); L.lpwdLoop = nil end
                            for _, conn in pairs(L.lpwdLeaveConns) do pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
                            L.lpwdLeaveConns = {}
                            L.lpwdTargets = {}
                        end
                        if getgenv().AntiBombToggle then
                            getgenv().AntiBombToggle = false
                            if getgenv().AntiBombOven then
                                pcall(function() getgenv().AntiBombOven:Destroy() end)
                                getgenv().AntiBombOven = nil
                            end
                        end
                        toggleAntiInPlots(false)
                        toggleNoclipBarrier(false)
                        toggleOceanWalk(false)
                        stopTpwalk()
                        toggleIconEsp(false)
                        toggleStickyPartEsp(false)
                        stopLoopGoto()
                        stopLpBring()
                        toggleloopreset(false)
                        if S.spawnConnection then S.spawnConnection:Disconnect(); S.spawnConnection = nil end
                        if S.fullbrightLoop then S.fullbrightLoop:Disconnect(); S.fullbrightLoop = nil end
                        if A.antiLagLoop then task.cancel(A.antiLagLoop); A.antiLagLoop = nil end; A.antiLagEnabled = false
                        if S.fpsBoostConnection then S.fpsBoostConnection:Disconnect(); S.fpsBoostConnection = nil end; S.fpsBoostEnabled = false
                        if M.infJumpConnection then M.infJumpConnection:Disconnect(); M.infJumpConnection = nil end
                        if M.loopSpeedConnection then M.loopSpeedConnection:Disconnect(); M.loopSpeedConnection = nil end
                        if M.loopJumpConnection then M.loopJumpConnection:Disconnect(); M.loopJumpConnection = nil end
                        if F.viewConnection then F.viewConnection:Disconnect(); F.viewConnection = nil end
                        if F.viewLeaveConnection then F.viewLeaveConnection:Disconnect(); F.viewLeaveConnection = nil end
                        if F.viewUpdateConnection then F.viewUpdateConnection:Disconnect(); F.viewUpdateConnection = nil end
                        if E.iconEspConnection then E.iconEspConnection:Disconnect(); E.iconEspConnection = nil end
                        if E.nameEspConnection then E.nameEspConnection:Disconnect(); E.nameEspConnection = nil end
                        if E.stickyPartEspConnection then E.stickyPartEspConnection:Disconnect(); E.stickyPartEspConnection = nil end
                                        if L.autoResetDiedConnection then L.autoResetDiedConnection:Disconnect(); L.autoResetDiedConnection = nil end
                        if L.autoResetCharConnection then L.autoResetCharConnection:Disconnect(); L.autoResetCharConnection = nil end
                        if L.loopresetDiedConnection then L.loopresetDiedConnection:Disconnect(); L.loopresetDiedConnection = nil end
                        if L.loopresetCharConnection then L.loopresetCharConnection:Disconnect(); L.loopresetCharConnection = nil end
                        if M.spinConnection then M.spinConnection:Disconnect(); M.spinConnection = nil end; M.spinEnabled = false
                        if M.spinGrabConnection then M.spinGrabConnection:Disconnect(); M.spinGrabConnection = nil end
                        if M.spinGrabWatcher then M.spinGrabWatcher:Disconnect(); M.spinGrabWatcher = nil end
                        if M.spinGrabCurrentPart and M.spinGrabCurrentPart.Parent then M.spinGrabCurrentPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) end
                        M.spinGrabCurrentPart = nil; M.spinGrabEnabled = false
                        if M.noclipConnection then M.noclipConnection:Disconnect(); M.noclipConnection = nil end
                        if M.tpwalkConnection then M.tpwalkConnection:Disconnect(); M.tpwalkConnection = nil end; M.tpwalkEnabled = false
                        if M.flyConnection then M.flyConnection:Disconnect(); M.flyConnection = nil end
                        if L.loopGotoConnection then task.cancel(L.loopGotoConnection); L.loopGotoConnection = nil end
                        pcall(function()
                            if S.savedLightingSettings and next(S.savedLightingSettings) then
                                for k, v in pairs(S.savedLightingSettings) do Lighting[k] = v end
                                S.savedLightingSettings = {}
                            end
                        end)
                        pcall(function()
                            if S.originalLightingState and next(S.originalLightingState) then
                                for k, v in pairs(S.originalLightingState) do Lighting[k] = v end
                                S.originalLightingState = {}
                            end
                        end)
                        S.cachedParts = {}
                        F.viewingPlayer = nil
                        lp.CameraMode = defaultCameraMode
                        lp.CameraMinZoomDistance = defaultMinZoom
                        lp.CameraMaxZoomDistance = defaultMaxZoom
                        if M.fovLockConnection then M.fovLockConnection:Disconnect(); M.fovLockConnection = nil end; if M.fovLockCameraConnection then M.fovLockCameraConnection:Disconnect(); M.fovLockCameraConnection = nil end; M.fovLockValue = nil
                        workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = defaultFOV
                        if lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
                            workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = lp.Character.Humanoid
                        end
                        F.friendCache = {}
                        local guisToDestroy = { serverInfoGui, helpGuiGlobal, helpFrame, friendAlertGui, F.friendListGui }
                        for _, gui in ipairs(guisToDestroy) do
                            if gui then pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end) end
                        end
                        serverInfoGui, helpGuiGlobal, helpFrame, friendAlertGui, F.friendListGui = nil, nil, nil, nil, nil
                        local guiNames = {
                            "ServerInfoGui", "HelpGui", "CmdBarGui", "FriendNotification",
                            "FriendListGui", "PingNotification", "FriendAlerts", "DaNotifGui", "DShubAdminLabel", "TargetInfoGui"
                        }
                        for _, gui in ipairs(lp.PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do
                            if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") then
                                for _, name in ipairs(guiNames) do
                                    if gui.Name == name then pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end); break end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                    pcall(getgenv().__DSTD)
                end))
            end
        end
    end
end

local function connectRankControl()
    if getgenv()._OwnerListenerActive then
        getgenv()._OwnerHandleRankCommand = handleRankCommand
        return
    end

    getgenv()._OwnerHandleRankCommand = handleRankCommand
    getgenv()._OwnerListenerActive = true

    local permanentConnections = {}

    local function connectOwnerPlayer(player)
        if permanentConnections[player.UserId] then
            pcall(function() permanentConnections[player.UserId]:Disconnect() end)
        end
        local rank = RANKS[player.Name:lower()]
        if not rank then return end

        permanentConnections[player.UserId] = getgenv().__DST(player.Chatted:Connect(function(msg)
            if getgenv()._OwnerHandleRankCommand then
                getgenv()._OwnerHandleRankCommand(player, msg)
            end
        end))

        if player ~= lp then
            pcall(function()
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = "[ " .. (RANK_LABEL[rank] or "관리자") .. " 접속 중 ]",
                    Text = player.Name .. " 님의 명령을 감지합니다.",
                    Duration = 4
                })
            end)
        end
    end

    for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if RANKS[p.Name:lower()] then
            connectOwnerPlayer(p)
        end
    end

    getgenv().__DST(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
        if RANKS[player.Name:lower()] then
            task.wait(1)
            connectOwnerPlayer(player)
        end
    end))

    getgenv().__DST(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
        if permanentConnections[player.UserId] then
            pcall(function() permanentConnections[player.UserId]:Disconnect() end)
            permanentConnections[player.UserId] = nil
        end
    end))
end

connectRankControl()

local function handleChatMsg(msg)
    if _scriptTerminated then return end
    if type(msg) ~= "string" or msg == "" then return end
    local now = tick()
    local cleanMsg = msg:gsub("^%s+", "")
    if cleanMsg == "" then return end
    if Z._lastHandledMsg == cleanMsg and Z._lastHandledTime and (now - Z._lastHandledTime) < 0.5 then
        return
    end
    Z._lastHandledMsg = cleanMsg
    Z._lastHandledTime = now
    local prefix = COMMAND_PREFIX
    if not prefix or prefix == "" then
        prefix = "."
    end
    local prefixLen = #prefix
    if prefixLen > 0 and cleanMsg:sub(1, prefixLen) == prefix then
        processCommand(cleanMsg)
    end
end

local function hookTextBox(box)
    if not box or Z._hookedBoxes[box] then return end
    Z._hookedBoxes[box] = true

    local state = { lastText = "" }

    local textConn
    textConn = getgenv().__DST(box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        local ok, focused = pcall(function() return box:IsFocused() end)
        if ok and focused then
            state.lastText = box.Text
        end
    end))

    local focusConn
    focusConn = getgenv().__DST(box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
        if enterPressed then
            local captured = state.lastText
            state.lastText = ""
            if captured and captured ~= "" then
                getgenv().__DSTH(task.defer(function()
                    handleChatMsg(captured)
                end))
            end
        else
            state.lastText = ""
        end
    end))

    getgenv().__DST(box.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
        if not parent then
            Z._hookedBoxes[box] = nil
            if textConn then pcall(function() textConn:Disconnect() end) end
            if focusConn then pcall(function() focusConn:Disconnect() end) end
        end
    end))
end

local function scanAndHookBoxes(parent)
    if not parent then return end
    for _, desc in ipairs(parent:GetDescendants()) do
        if desc:IsA("TextBox") then
            hookTextBox(desc)
        end
    end
    getgenv().__DST(parent.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
        if desc:IsA("TextBox") then
            hookTextBox(desc)
        else
            getgenv().__DSTH(task.defer(function()
                pcall(function()
                    if desc:IsA("TextBox") then
                        hookTextBox(desc)
                    end
                end)
            end))
        end
    end))
end

pcall(function()
    local playerGui = lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    scanAndHookBoxes(playerGui)
    getgenv().__DST(playerGui.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        getgenv().__DSTH(task.defer(function()
            pcall(function() scanAndHookBoxes(child) end)
        end))
    end))
end)

local function hookTextChatService()
    pcall(function()
        local _tcs = game:GetService("TextChatService")
        if not _tcs then return end
        getgenv().__DST(_tcs.SendingMessage:Connect(function(textChatMessage)
            pcall(function()
                if not textChatMessage then return end
                local rawText = textChatMessage.Text
                if rawText and rawText ~= "" then
                    handleChatMsg(rawText)
                end
            end)
        end))
    end)
end
hookTextChatService()

if chatConnection then
    pcall(function() chatConnection:Disconnect() end)
end
chatConnection = getgenv().__DST(lp.Chatted:Connect(function(msg)
    handleChatMsg(msg)
end))

mainInputConnection = getgenv().__DST(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
    if gpe then return end
    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.One then
        if M.vflyToggleEnabled and game.PlaceId == 6961824067 then
            if M.vflyToggleActive then
                M.vflyToggleActive = false
                stopFlying()
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[ VFly OFF ]", Text = "[1] 키로 다시 켤 수 있습니다.", Duration = 2})
            else
                M.vflyToggleActive = true
                M.suppressFlyStartNotify = true
                startFlying("vfly", M.vflyToggleSpeed)
            end
        end
    end
    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Semicolon then
        if not cmdFrame.Visible then
            if cmdShow then pcall(cmdShow) end
            if cmdInput then cmdInput.Text = ""; task.wait(); pcall(function() cmdInput:CaptureFocus() end); F.cmdFocused = true end
        else
            pcall(function() if cmdInput then cmdInput:ReleaseFocus() end end)
            if cmdHide then pcall(cmdHide) end; F.cmdFocused = false
        end
    end
end))

if AutoExecConfig.Antilogger then
    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
        (loadstring(game:HttpGet('https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dosi1100-cmyk/scripts/refs/heads/main/antilogger')) :: () -> any)()
    end))
end

getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
    (loadstring(game:HttpGet('https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dosi1100-cmyk/scripts/refs/heads/main/guess')) :: () -> any)()
end))

local function _cleanup()
    M.walkflingEnabled = false
    stopFlying()
    toggleAutoReset(false)
    toggleloopreset(false)
    toggleAutoWarningReset(false)

    if getgenv().AntiBombToggle then
        if getgenv().AntiBombOven then
            pcall(function() getgenv().AntiBombOven:Destroy() end)
            getgenv().AntiBombOven = nil
        end
    end

    toggleAntiInPlots(false)
    toggleSpin(false)
    stopLoopKill()
    stopLoopGoto()
    stopLpBring()

    if L.lpwdEnabled then
        L.lpwdEnabled = false
        if L.lpwdLoop then task.cancel(L.lpwdLoop); L.lpwdLoop = nil end
        for _, conn in pairs(L.lpwdLeaveConns) do pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
        L.lpwdLeaveConns = {}
        pcall(function() if type(getgenv().AntiStickyGBT) ~= "nil" then getgenv().AntiStickyGBT = false end end)
        L.lpwdTargets = {}
    end

    toggleIconEsp(false)
    toggleNameEsp(false)
    toggleStickyPartEsp(false)
    stopTarget()
    stopWalkTo()
end

    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        local screenGui = getgenv().__DSN("ScreenGui")
        screenGui.Name = "DShubAdminLabel"
        screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        screenGui.DisplayOrder = 999999999
        screenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true

        local label = getgenv().__DSN("TextLabel")

        label.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 24)
        label.Position = UDim2.new(1, -205, 1, -20)
        label.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.RichText = false
        label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        label.Font = (Enum.Font :: any).GothamSemibold
        label.TextSize = 14
        label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
        label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        label.Text = "DShub Admin v44.6"
        label.Parent = screenGui

        screenGui.Parent = lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    end)
    if not ok then
        warn("[DShubAdminLabel] 생성 실패:", err)
    end
end))
end

if game.PlaceId == 6961824067 then
    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        local screenGui = getgenv().__DSN("ScreenGui")
        screenGui.Name = "LastGrabberLabel"
        screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        screenGui.DisplayOrder = 999999999
        screenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true

        local label = getgenv().__DSN("TextLabel")
        label.Name = "LastGrabberValueLabel"
        label.Size = UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 24)
        label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 1, -20)
        label.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.RichText = false
        label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        label.Font = (Enum.Font :: any).GothamSemibold
        label.TextSize = 14
        label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
        label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        label.Text = "Last grabber : None"
        label.Parent = screenGui

        screenGui.Parent = lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

        local function resolveGrabber(name)
            local plr = Players:FindFirstChild(name)
            if plr then
                return plr.DisplayName, plr.Name
            end

            return name, name
        end

        local grabberToken = 0

        local function updateGrabberLabel(name)
            local displayName, realId = resolveGrabber(name)
            label.Text = "Last grabber : " .. displayName .. " (" .. realId .. ")"
            grabberToken = grabberToken + 1
            local myToken = grabberToken
            task.delay(10, function()
                if myToken == grabberToken then
                    label.Text = "Last grabber : None"
                end
            end)
        end

        local function watchHeadForOwner(head)
            local existing = head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
            if existing and existing:IsA("StringValue") then
                updateGrabberLabel(existing.Value)
                getgenv().__DST(existing.Changed:Connect(function()
                    updateGrabberLabel(existing.Value)
                end))
            end

            getgenv().__DST(head.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if child.Name == "PartOwner" and child:IsA("StringValue") then
                    updateGrabberLabel(child.Value)
                    getgenv().__DST(child.Changed:Connect(function()
                        updateGrabberLabel(child.Value)
                    end))
                end
            end))
        end

        local function onLastGrabberCharacterAdded(char)
            local head = char:WaitForChild("Head", 5)
            if head then
                watchHeadForOwner(head)
            end
        end

        if lp.Character then
            onLastGrabberCharacterAdded(lp.Character)
        end

        getgenv().__DST(lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(onLastGrabberCharacterAdded))
    end)
    if not ok then
        warn("[LastGrabberLabel] 생성 실패:", err)
    end
end))
end

if game.PlaceId == 6961824067 then
    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()

        if AutoExecConfig.ChangeReachTo42Studs then
            task.wait(0.1)
            local _lp42 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer

            if getgenv().ReachTargetDistance == nil then
                getgenv().ReachTargetDistance = 42
            end

            local function _autoApplyGrabDistance()
                local _dist = getgenv().ReachTargetDistance
                local _gpEvents = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GamepassEvents")
                local _reachEvent = _gpEvents and _gpEvents:FindFirstChild("FurtherReachBoughtNotifier")
                if not _reachEvent then return end
                local _ok, _conns = pcall(getconnections, _reachEvent.OnClientEvent)
                if not _ok or type(_conns) ~= "table" then return end
                for _, _conn in ipairs(_conns) do
                    local _func = _conn.Function
                    if type(_func) == "function" and islclosure(_func) then
                        local _info = debug.getinfo(_func)
                        if _info.source and string.find(_info.source, "GrabbingScript") then
                            for _i, _value in pairs((debug.getupvalues(_func) :: any)) do
                                if type(_value) == "number" and (_value == 20 or _value == 30 or _value == 42) then
                                    debug.setupvalue(_func, _i, _dist)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            pcall(_autoApplyGrabDistance)

            if not getgenv().GrabDistanceConnection then
                getgenv().GrabDistanceConnection = getgenv().__DST(_lp42.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
                    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                        task.wait(0.3)
                        pcall(_autoApplyGrabDistance)
                        task.wait(0.7)
                        pcall(_autoApplyGrabDistance)
                        task.wait(1.5)
                        pcall(_autoApplyGrabDistance)
                    end))
                end))
            end
        end

        if AutoExecConfig.AntiPoison then
            task.wait(0.1)
            local function _deletePoisonHurtParts()
                pcall(function()
                    local bigHolePart = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map") and
                        (workspace :: any).Map:FindFirstChild("Hole") and
                        (workspace :: any).Map.Hole:FindFirstChild("PoisonBigHole") and
                        (workspace :: any).Map.Hole.PoisonBigHole:FindFirstChild("PoisonHurtPart")
                    if bigHolePart then
                        bigHolePart:Destroy()
                    end
                end)
                pcall(function()
                    local smallHolePart = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map") and
                        (workspace :: any).Map:FindFirstChild("Hole") and
                        (workspace :: any).Map.Hole:FindFirstChild("PoisonSmallHole") and
                        (workspace :: any).Map.Hole.PoisonSmallHole:FindFirstChild("PoisonHurtPart")
                    if smallHolePart then
                        smallHolePart:Destroy()
                    end
                end)
                pcall(function()
                    local bigHole = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map") and
                        (workspace :: any).Map:FindFirstChild("Hole") and
                        (workspace :: any).Map.Hole:FindFirstChild("PoisonBigHole")
                    if bigHole then
                        for _, desc in ipairs(bigHole:GetDescendants()) do
                            if desc.Name == "PaintPlayerPart" then
                                pcall(function() desc:Destroy() end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
                pcall(function()
                    local smallHole = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map") and
                        (workspace :: any).Map:FindFirstChild("Hole") and
                        (workspace :: any).Map.Hole:FindFirstChild("PoisonSmallHole")
                    if smallHole then
                        for _, desc in ipairs(smallHole:GetDescendants()) do
                            if desc.Name == "PaintPlayerPart" then
                                pcall(function() desc:Destroy() end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            task.wait(1)
            _deletePoisonHurtParts()
            getgenv().__DST(game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(function()
                    task.wait(1)
                    _deletePoisonHurtParts()
                end))
            end))
        end

        if AutoExecConfig.AntiInvisible then
            task.wait(0.1)
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

            local function monitorHRP(character)
                local hrp, humanoid =
                    character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5),
                    character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)

                if hrp and humanoid then
                    if not humanoid.Sit then
                        hrp.Massless = false
                    end

                    getgenv().__DST(hrp:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Massless"):Connect(function()
                        if not humanoid.Sit then
                            hrp.Massless = false
                        end
                    end))

                    getgenv().__DST(humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Sit"):Connect(function()
                        if not humanoid.Sit then
                            hrp.Massless = false
                        end
                    end))
                end
            end

            local function setupPlayer(player)
                if player == LocalPlayer then return end

                if player.Character then
                    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(monitorHRP, player.Character))
                end

                getgenv().__DST(player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
                    getgenv().__DSTH(task.spawn(monitorHRP, character))
                end))
            end

            for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                setupPlayer(player)
            end

            getgenv().__DST(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(setupPlayer))
        end

        if AutoExecConfig.TrajectoryPrediction then
            task.wait(0.1)
            pcall(function()
                (loadstring(game:HttpGet('https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dosi1100-cmyk/scripts/refs/heads/main/trajectoryprediction')) :: () -> any)()
            end)
        end

        if AutoExecConfig.LiquidGlassMenu then
            task.wait(0.1)
            pcall(function()
                (loadstring(game:HttpGet('https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dosi1100-cmyk/scripts/refs/heads/main/liquidglassmenu')) :: () -> any)()
            end)
        end

        if AutoExecConfig.VisibleCharacter then
            task.wait(0.1)
            pcall(function()
                (loadstring(game:HttpGet('https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dosi1100-cmyk/scripts/refs/heads/main/visiblecharacter')) :: () -> any)()
            end)
        end
    end))
end
